项目一 认识餐饮服务

Project 1 Cognition of Catering Services
项目概述Project Overview
As is well known, the catering industry belongs to the consumer service industry and is quite different from other industries. Therefore, before learning and engaging in catering services, it is necessary for us to understand the basic concepts and fundamental knowledge of catering services; only in this way can we provide quality service to guests. Catering service requires a cordial and enthusiastic attitude. Service personnel must always put guests first, making them feel at home. The quality of catering service is the soul of a restaurant and is also its main product for sale. As catering practitioners, we must understand the true meaning of catering service and its importance to restaurant operation. To perfect their work, catering practitioners must possess the professional knowledge and basic qualities of catering service. Each stage from the beginning to the end of service is closely linked, which relates to the formulation and implementation of the restaurant's service quality. At present, common catering service methods can be divided into Chinese food service and western food service, each with detailed classifications and characteristics.
众所周知，餐饮业属于消费性服务业，与其他行业相比有着很大的差异。因此，在学习并从事餐饮服务业之前，我们有必要了解餐饮服务的基本概念、基础知识，只有这样我们才能为客人提供优质的服务。 餐饮服务需要亲切热人的态度，服务人员要时时刻刻为客人着想，使客人有宾至如归的感觉。餐饮服务质量是餐厅的灵魂，更是餐厅所销售的主要产品。作为餐饮从业者，我们要了解餐饮服务的真谛，了解服务对餐厅经营的重要性。 餐饮服务从业者要想将自己的工作做到完美，就必须具备餐饮服务的专业知识和基本素质。从开始提供服务到服务结束的每一个阶段都是环环相扣的，这关系到餐厅服务品质的制定和实施。 目前，常见的餐饮服务方式可统分为中餐服务方式和西餐服务方式，每种服务方式各有详细的分类和特点。
项目目标Project Objectives
项目目标 Project Objectives
类别Category内容Content
知识目标Knowledge Objectives了解餐饮服务的概念及特性；了解餐饮服务品质的内涵，掌握从事餐饮服务应具备的基本技能；掌握中、西餐的服务方式。Understand the concept and characteristics of catering services; understand the connotation of catering service quality; master the basic skills required for catering services; master the service methods of Chinese and western food.
技能目标Skill Objectives能够根据餐厅的规模设计餐厅的组织架构。Be able to design the organizational structure of a restaurant according to its size.
素质目标Quality Objectives能够具备从事餐饮服务所需要的基本素质与知识。Be able to possess the basic qualities and knowledge required for catering services.
思政目标Ideological Objectives坚定文化自信、民族自信；树立自我提高、爱岗敬业意识；激发对餐饮行业的热爱，树立餐饮服务意识。Strengthen cultural confidence and national self-confidence; establish awareness of self-improvement and dedication to work; stimulate love for the catering industry and build a sense of catering service.

任务1 餐饮服务概述

Task 1: An Overview of Catering Services
案例导入Case Introduction
Mr. Wang was quite satisfied when he first stayed at the Oriental Hotel in Thailand, so when he went to Thailand on a business trip for the second time, he still chose the Oriental Hotel. When he arrived at the restaurant, the waiter greeted him: "Mr. Wang, welcome back! Would you like your old seat by the window?" Mr. Wang was very surprised, thinking that although it was not his first time dining there, it had been more than a year since his last visit—how could the waiter remember him so well? Seeing his surprised look, the waiter explained voluntarily: "I just checked the computer records. You had breakfast on June last year at the second window near the front." Mr. Wang said excitedly: "The old seat! Good!" Later, the hotel sent Mr. Wang a greeting card with a letter, saying that all the staff of the Oriental Hotel missed him very much and hoped to see him again. Mr. Wang was so moved that he decided that whenever he returned to Thailand he would stay at the Oriental Hotel, and would persuade all his friends to do the same. Thinking and discussion: What inspiration can we draw from this case?
王先生第一次入住泰国东方饭店时感觉很不错，所以，他第二次到泰国出差时仍选择东方饭店。当王先生来到餐厅时，服务员迎上来："王先生，欢迎回来！还是靠窗的老位子吗？"王先生感到十分吃惊，心想尽管他不是第一次在这里吃饭，但最近一次也有一年多了，难道这里的服务员记忆力这么好？ 看到他惊讶的样子，服务员主动解释说："我刚刚查了电脑记录，您在去年的6月，在靠近第二个窗口的位子上用过早餐。" 王先生听后兴奋地说道："老位子！好！" 后来，饭店给王先生寄来一张贺卡，并附上一封信，表示东方饭店全体员工十分想念他，希望能再次见到他。王先生当时激动得热泪盈眶，发誓再到泰国去，一定要住在东方饭店，并且要说服所有的朋友像他一样。 思考讨论：从这个案例中我们可以得到什么启示？
知识准备Knowledge Preparation

The Concept and Characteristics of Catering Services

The catering industry sells not only meals and drinks, but also restaurant space, decorations, equipment and services.

Service is an attitude of knowing and providing what guests need at a specific time and place to make them feel at home. The service impulse must be from the heart, manifested outwardly in concrete appearance.

Service is a kind of feeling to the customer. When the customer is in the restaurant, the demand for the service is very extensive and abstract, and the evaluation of the service will be different with the change of the environment and mood. Experts have given different accounts of the nature of the service. Here we summarize the following four points:

(1) Intangibility — The intangible service refers to the service that is not specific.
(2) Heterogeneity — The heterogeneity of service means that the service provided to the guests by different service personnel will be different, and the service provided by the same waiter to different customers also will be different.
(3) Indivisibility — Service providers and equipment must come together with the customers' demand, with its indivisible characteristics.
(4) Perishability — Service is fleeting and cannot be stored.

The Quality of Catering Services

The quality of catering services includes: service efficiency, timely service, good communication with guests, consistency of service, good service attitude, professional knowledge of service personnel, flexibility of service, and establishment of customer feedback system.

(1) Service efficiency: For restaurant service personnel, the performance of a rapid and smooth rhythm in the work can make guests feel comfortable. That the service personnel make the corresponding preparation work before the restaurant is open and the team members cooperate well can make the working efficiency improve, thus smoothly completing the service reception work.

(2) Timely service: A good service personnel should be able to observe and predict the potential needs of guests, and deliver appropriate services initiatively at the proper time.

(3) Good communication with guests: The ways of communication between service personnel and guests will vary according to the types of restaurant. At the same time, the service personnel should adjust their communication methods according to different types of guests. If guests deliberately maintain a distance from service personnel and do not want to do more communication, then service personnel should not disturb guests as far as possible.

(4) Consistency of service: If guests like the restaurant's food, environment and service, then they will come to the restaurant again. If the restaurant can not provide a consistent standard of food and service, the guests will gradually leave. Consistency of service means that every guest can be provided with consistent high quality services at every moment.

(5) Good service attitude: Good service attitude includes active attitude, courteous service, proficient service skills and so on.

(6) Professional knowledge of service personnel: The service personnel must have a good understanding of the products provided in the restaurant in order to introduce or answer guests' questions.

(7) Flexibility of service: Although restaurant positions have their own standard operating procedures (SOP), sometimes service rules must be adjusted to the actual situation. The service personnel should adjust the service strategy according to the actual situation.

(8) Establishment of customer feedback system: Restaurant should establish customer feedback mechanism and system, and actively investigate and respond to customer's feedback which should be the basis for service improvement.

Organizational Structure of Food and Beverage Department

餐饮服务的概念及特性

餐饮业所销售的不仅是餐食和饮料，同时包括餐厅的空间、装潢、设备以及服务。

服务是一种态度，是在特定的时间和地点及时去了解并提供客人所需，让客人有宾至如归的感觉。服务必须是发自内心、表现于外的具体呈现。

服务是客人的一种感受。当客人在餐厅时，对服务的需求非常广泛而抽象，对服务的评价也会随环境、心境的变化而有所不同。对于服务的特性，各专家给出了不同的说法，在这里我们总结为以下4点：

（一）无形性：服务的无形性是指服务不是具象的。
（二）异质性：服务的异质性是指不同服务人员对客人提供的服务会有所不同，同一服务人员对不同客人提供的服务也会有所不同。
（三）不可分割性：提供服务的人和设备必须与客人的需求同时出现，服务有着不可分割的特性。
（四）易逝性：服务是稍纵即逝的，是无法储存的。

餐饮服务的品质

餐饮服务的品质包括：服务效率、适时服务、与客人沟通良好、服务一致性、良好的服务态度、具备专业知识、服务具有弹性、建立顾客反馈系统。

（一）服务效率：对餐厅服务人员而言，在工作中表现快速而顺畅能够使客人感到舒适自在。在餐厅营业前做好相应的准备工作，团队成员配合良好，能使工作效率提高，从而顺利完成服务接待工作。
（二）适时服务：一名优秀的服务人员要能够随时观察并预知客人的潜在需求，主动并适时地提供适当的服务。
（三）与客人沟通良好：服务人员与客人的沟通方式会因为餐厅类型不同而有所差异，同时，服务人员应针对不同类型的客人适时调整自己的沟通方式。若客人刻意保持与服务人员的距离不愿多沟通，那么服务人员就尽量不要打扰客人。
（四）服务一致性：若客人喜欢餐厅的食物、环境、服务，就会再度光临餐厅。如果餐厅无法提供一致性、高水准的食物和服务，客源就会逐渐流失。服务的一致性是指针对每一位客人在每一个时刻，均能提供一致的高品质服务。
（五）良好的服务态度：良好的服务态度包括服务人员态度主动积极、服务殷勤周到、服务技巧熟练等。
（六）具备专业知识：服务人员必须对餐厅所提供的产品进行充分了解，才能介绍或回答客人提出的问题。
（七）服务具有弹性：虽然餐厅各岗位均有各自的标准作业流程（SOP），但有时候服务规则必须根据实际情况做出调整。服务人员应根据实际情况来调整服务策略。
（八）建立顾客反馈系统：餐厅应建立顾客反馈机制和系统，对于顾客反馈的问题进行积极调查并做出回应，以此作为改进服务的依据。

餐饮部组织架构

餐饮部组织架构见表（餐饮部组织架构表），各岗位的直接上级与岗位职责如上表所示。

任务实施Task Implementation

Step 1: The teacher introduces the concept, characteristics and qualities of the catering service, and introduces the organizational structure of different types of restaurants and the corresponding job responsibilities.

Step 2: The teacher introduces this section and selects one group (each group should choose one case) to do the simulation exercise.

Step 3: According to the size and nature of the restaurant given by the teacher, the organizational structure of the restaurant should be designed by the group (5 people in 1 group).

第一步：教师介绍什么是餐饮服务、餐饮服务的特性及餐饮服务的品质，并介绍不同类型餐厅的组织架构及相应的岗位职责。

第二步：教师导入本节内容，并抽取1个小组（每个小组选其中1个案例）进行现场模拟表演。

第三步：根据教师所给餐厅的规模与性质，以小组（5人1组）为单位相应改变餐厅的组织架构。

拓展阅读Extended Reading

The Role of Smile Service in Hotels

A smile, like the eyes, is a silent language and a "lubricant" in interpersonal communication. It is the outward expression of pleasant feelings, and a symbol of kindness, friendliness and beauty. A measured smile, combined with graceful manners, is often more charming than spoken words, and on certain occasions achieves the effect of "silence speaks louder than words".

The founder of the Hilton Hotels once said: "First-class equipment is important, but the smile of a first-class waiter is even more important. Without the beautiful smile of the service staff, a hotel is like a garden that has lost the spring sunshine and breeze. If I were a customer, I would rather stay in a hotel with worn carpets where smiles can be found everywhere, than walk into a hotel with first-class equipment but no smiles." It was precisely the charm of smiles that enabled the Hilton Hotels to grow rapidly during the Great Depression of the United States in the 1930s.

The Oriental Hotel in Bangkok, Thailand, has been ranked among the "World's Top Ten Hotels" several times; one of the secrets of its success is to include "a beaming smile" in its reception standards.

A smile is both a science and an art, and an important part of quality service. It plays a very important role in enhancing the reputation of a hotel and obtaining the best economic and social benefits.

(1) A smile is a symbol of self-confidence. To fully respect oneself and demonstrate the value of one's existence, one must pay attention to strengthening self-image; self-confidence is fully reflected in a smile.
(2) A smile is the full expression of etiquette. A knowledgeable, courteous and polite person will surely respect others. Even a stranger will not hesitate to offer a smile as a gift to others.
(3) A smile reflects harmonious coexistence. It shows friendliness and closeness between people.
(4) A smile is a sign of mental health. It reflects a calm, optimistic and open state of mind.

1. In the text, what is a smile compared to in interpersonal communication?
It is compared to a "lubricant" in interpersonal communication — a silent language that helps people get along.
2. According to the founder of the Hilton Hotels, what is more important than first-class equipment?
The smile of a first-class waiter / service staff is more important than first-class equipment.
3. How did smiles help the Hilton Hotels during the 1930s?
The charm of smiles enabled the Hilton Hotels to achieve rapid growth during the U.S. economic depression of the 1930s.
4. Why did the Oriental Hotel in Bangkok list "a beaming smile" as a reception standard?
Because it was one of the secrets of its success in being ranked among the world's top ten hotels.
5. According to the text, what four things does a smile symbolize?
A smile symbolizes self-confidence, the full expression of etiquette, harmonious coexistence, and mental health.

任务2 餐饮服务人员素质要求

Task 2: The Quality Requirements of Food and Beverage Service Staff
案例导入Case Introduction
Manager Wang has worked in the hotel industry for 20 years. Starting from a service position and working step by step, he is now the general manager of a five-star hotel. During his 20-year career, he kept improving his educational level and comprehensive qualities — from a junior-high education to a self-taught bachelor's degree. He also studied English and Japanese enthusiastically, and was warm, thoughtful and meticulous toward others, winning unanimous praise from guests, colleagues and superiors. Thinking and discussion: If you were a guest, what kind of image would you expect a restaurant waiter to have?
王经理从事酒店工作20年，从服务员开始一步一步做起，现在担任某五星级酒店总经理。在20年的工作经历中，他不断提高自己的学历层次和综合素质，从只有初中学历到自考本科文凭，并且还热忱自学英语、日语，对人热情、周到、细致，受到客人、同事、上司的一致好评。 思考讨论：如果你是客人，你希望看到餐厅服务员具备什么形象？
知识准备Knowledge Preparation

Grooming

Once catering service begins it cannot be returned or exchanged, which requires every service staff to pay attention to their image and behavior, possessing decent appearance and proper service etiquette.

(1) Grooming standards:
a. Employees should be full of energy, with natural expression and a smile.
b. The eyes should show enthusiasm, courtesy, kindness and sincerity.
c. When you speak, you should have a peaceful and friendly tone which is not over-exaggerated.
d. When you talk with guests, you should look naturally and do not look up and down.
e. Neither overbearing nor servile, and be easy to deal with troubles.

(2) Standard behavior requirements — Clothes:
a. All restaurant staff should wear clean, ironed and fully accessorized uniforms.
b. The uniform should be clean, ironed, with no wrinkles, damages or stains, and do not roll up your sleeves or trouser legs.
c. The name tag should be on the upper left of the chest, level and not skewed.
d. The uniform buttons should be complete and not loose.
e. Male staff should wear dark (black or dark blue) socks; female staff should wear flesh-colored silk stockings. Socks should not be torn and should be changed daily.
f. Shoes should be clean, not deformed or broken, with no stain or dust; shoelaces should be tied and not drag on the ground.
g. Male staff's hair should be short and combed; sideburns are not allowed, and hair should not cover the neck, ears or shoulders.
h. Female staff with long hair should coil it up; the bun should not be too high or too low (no higher than the collar). Short hair should not fall below the collar; loose hair should be set with hairspray. Hair in front of guests must not block the eyes; hair color should be natural, not odd.

Manners & etiquette is the posture and presence of a person in action, which is as important to a hotel employee as grooming.

Standing posture:
a. The standing posture should be natural and upright, not leaning, looking straight ahead.
b. The body should be upright; male staff's feet are naturally separated to shoulder width; female staff close their feet naturally.
c. Keep shoulders flat, chest slightly out and abdomen in.
d. The restaurant service personnel should cross hands naturally, with the right hand holding the left hand's wrist.
e. Never cross arms on the chest, stand akimbo, or put hands in pockets.

Walking posture:
a. While walking, keep the body upright and lean slightly forward; keep the head upright, look straight ahead and relax the shoulders.
b. Swing both arms naturally and walk with a gentle pace.
c. Do not step too far, and do not walk too fast.

Service Qualities of Restaurant Staff

With fierce competition, guests pay more attention to F&B service quality. The improvement of catering service quality depends on high-quality staff, who need not only food and beverage knowledge but also professional qualities and abilities.

(1) Requirements of attitudes — Service attitudes depend on the staff's initiative, creativity, positivity, responsibility and quality.
a. Initiative: Catering service personnel should firmly establish the "guest first, service first" professional awareness. Whatever guests need, staff should take the initiative to solve the problem immediately after noticing it, being attentive and doing the service before guests ask.
b. Enthusiasm: F&B service staff should love the job and the guests, treating guests as relatives and friends, always smiling, full of spirit, kind and helpful.
c. Patience: F&B service staff should be patient with guests, try to understand their consumption psychology, listen to opinions and suggestions, and when in conflict with guests, respect them, be self-disciplined, calm and patient.
d. Consideration: Before work, staff should prepare fully and make detailed plans; during service, observe and meet guests' needs; at the end, solicit opinions and give feedback to improve service.

(2) Requirements of service knowledge — F&B service staff should have a broad scope of knowledge.
a. Basic knowledge: staff regulations, service consciousness, etiquette, professional morality & safety, service psychology and foreign language.
b. Professional knowledge: duty responsibilities, work procedures, rotating forms, management regulations, facility operation and protection, restaurant services & operation time, communication skills.
c. Relating knowledge: religion, philosophy, aesthetics, literature, art, law, history and geography, customs and etiquette, folk culture, tourist attractions and traffic, etc.

(3) Requirements of skills:
a. Language skills: language should be civilized, polite, clear and concise; grasp Mandarin and master foreign languages such as English and Japanese when necessary.
b. Communication skills: good communication reduces complaints; serve different people with proper words.
c. Reaction capabilities: flexible and calm; able to settle crises and complaints; note that different gender, age, nationality and habits lead to different needs.
d. Promotion skills: recommend dishes according to guests' interest, habits and consumption level to enhance economic benefits.
e. Technical skills: skills shown in service improve efficiency, guarantee standards and please guests.
f. Observation ability: keenly observe and timely meet guests' needs based on their satisfaction.
g. Memorizing ability: remember guests' preferences to provide personalized service on return visits.
h. Self-discipline ability: self-control at work; know what to do and not do, where and when.
i. Obeying and coordinating ability: unconditionally obey the direct supervisor and satisfy guests' reasonable requests within traditional morality and socialist spiritual civilization.

(4) Physical quality:
a. Be healthy: regular check-ups and a health certificate; those unfit for the work should be reassigned.
b. Be strong: the work is physically demanding, requiring leg, arm and waist strength; staff must have a robust physique.

仪容仪表

餐饮服务一经开始就无法退换，这就要求每位服务人员注意自己的形象举止，要拥有仪容端庄、仪表得体的服务礼仪。

（一）仪容规范标准：
1. 员工应精神饱满，表情自然，面带微笑。
2. 眼神应显露出热情、礼貌、友善和真诚。
3. 说话时应语气平和，语调亲切，不可过分夸张。
4. 与客人交谈时，目光应自然平视，不应上下打量客人。
5. 不卑不亢，从容应对麻烦。

（二）仪表规范标准——服装：
1. 所有餐厅员工应穿着干净、熨烫平整并配齐饰物的制服。
2. 制服应干净、熨烫、无皱褶、无破损和污渍，不得卷起衣袖或裤腿。
3. 工号牌要佩戴在上衣的左胸上方，工号牌水平，不得倾斜。
4. 制服扣子应齐全、无松动。
5. 男员工着深色（黑色或深蓝色）袜子，女员工着肉色丝袜，袜子不应有破洞或抽丝，应每天换洗。
6. 鞋子应保持干净、无变形、无破损，不得有污点、灰尘；鞋带要系好，不可拖拉于地面。
7. 男员工头发应短并梳理整齐，不可留鬓角，头发不可遮住颈、耳或肩。
8. 女员工长发应盘起，发髻不宜过高或过低，以不过衣领为标准。短发不能过领，散乱头发应用发胶定型。客前头发不可挡住视线，头发颜色应自然，不要另类。

仪态是人在行动中的姿态和风度，对酒店员工而言与仪容仪表同等重要。

站姿：
1. 站姿应自然挺拔，不倚不靠，目视前方。
2. 身体直立，男员工双脚自然分开，基本与肩同宽，女员工双脚自然并拢。
3. 双肩平齐，挺胸，收腹。
4. 餐饮服务员双手应自然交叉在前，右手轻握左手的手腕。
5. 站立时双手不可抱在胸前、叉腰，双手不可插在口袋中。

走姿：
1. 行走时上体要保持正直，身体重心可稍向前倾，头部端正，双目平视，肩部放松。
2. 两臂自然摆动，步履轻盈。
3. 步伐不宜过大，行走不宜过快。

餐厅服务人员基本素质

随着竞争日趋激烈，客人对餐饮服务质量的要求越来越高，而餐饮服务质量的提高有赖于高素质的员工。在餐饮业工作，不仅需要具备菜肴与饮料知识，还需要具备一定的素质和能力。

（一）服务态度要求：服务态度取决于员工的主动性、创造性、积极性、责任感和素质。
1. 主动：餐饮服务人员应牢固树立"宾客至上、服务第一"的专业意识，发现客人需要后立即主动、及时解决，做到腿勤、口勤、手勤、脚勤、心勤，把服务工作做在客人开口之前。
2. 热情：服务人员热爱本职工作，热爱服务对象，像对待亲友一样为客人服务，做到面带微笑、精神饱满、诚恳待人、助人为乐。
3. 耐心：服务人员应有耐心，揣摩客人消费心理，耐心解答问题，虚心听取意见，与客人发生矛盾时尊重客人、加强自律、心平气和、耐心解释。
4. 周到：服务前做好充分准备，制定细致周到的计划；服务时仔细观察，及时发现并满足需求；服务结束时认真征求意见并及时反馈。

（二）服务知识要求：餐饮服务人员应具有较广的知识面。
1. 基础知识：员工守则、服务意识、礼仪礼貌、职业道德与安全、服务心理学和外语知识等。
2. 专业知识：岗位职责、工作程序、运转表单、管理制度、设施设备的使用与保养、饭店的服务项目及营业时间、沟通技巧等。
3. 相关知识：宗教知识、哲学、美学、文学、艺术、法律，各国的历史地理、习俗和礼仪、民俗与旅游景点及交通等。

（三）服务能力要求：
1. 语言能力：语言文明、礼貌、清楚、简明，提倡讲普通话，掌握英语、日语等外语。
2. 沟通能力：良好的沟通能力可减少投诉与问题，沟通因人而异、语言得当。
3. 反应能力：灵活应变、遇事冷静、及时妥善处理；注意客人的性别、年龄、国籍、爱好不同会导致不同需求。
4. 推销能力：根据客人兴趣、习惯和消费水平推销，提高餐饮部经济效益。
5. 技术能力：服务中显现的技巧与能力，提高效率、保证规格标准、给客人赏心悦目的感受。
6. 观察能力：敏锐观察客人需求并及时满足。
7. 记忆能力：记住客人需求信息，再次光临时提供有针对性的个性化服务。
8. 自律能力：工作中的自我控制能力，明确何时何地能做什么、不能做什么。
9. 服从与协调能力：无条件服从直接上级指令；服从有度，满足客人符合传统道德和社会主义精神文明的合理需求。

（四）身体素质要求：
1. 身体健康：定期体检，取得健康证；不适宜者调离岗位。
2. 体格健壮：劳动强度大，须有腿力、臂力和腰力，体魄强健方能胜任。

任务实施Task Implementation

Step 1: Before class, the teacher gives students 8–10 cases, and the students are divided into 8 groups. Each case expresses one requirement of ability of F&B department staff. The teacher asks students to discuss and analyze, and each group summarizes the corresponding ability requirement of each case.

Step 2: The teacher introduces this section and selects one group (each group chooses one case) to do the simulation exercise.

Step 3: The teacher gives the lecture and leads the students to analyze the cases and gives the correct answers. Each group carries on the comparison inspection.

第一步：课前教师给学生8—10个案例，学生被分为8个小组，每个案例对应一项"餐饮从业人员的服务能力要求"内容，要求学生以小组形式讨论分析，各组总结出各个案例对应的能力要求。

第二步：教师导入本节内容，并抽取1个小组（每个小组选其中1个案例）进行现场模拟表演。

第三步：教师讲授本节内容，并带领学生分析案例，给出正确答案。各小组进行对照检查。

拓展阅读Extended Reading

Effective Ways of Team Building

1. Setting goals — This emphasizes the importance of clear individual and team goals. Team members participate in action plans to identify ways of success and failure and achieve goals, so as to enhance members' motivation and foster their sense of ownership. By defining concrete outcomes, the whole team can measure its progress.

2. Assigning roles — Team members are assigned different roles; in the process they develop an understanding of the importance of organizational structure. It also emphasizes members' independence and the importance of each member focusing on their own role for the success of the whole group.

3. Solving problems — Team members identify the key problems of the team and work together to find solutions, thereby enhancing their critical thinking skills.

4. Interpersonal relationships — This aims to enhance members' cooperation abilities, such as giving support, accepting help, and communicating and sharing. Teams with fewer interpersonal conflicts usually function more effectively than others. A facilitator helps members build an atmosphere of mutual trust and open communication.

1. Why is setting clear goals important for a team?
It enhances team members' motivation and fosters a sense of ownership; the whole team can measure progress against specific outcomes.
2. What do team members learn by being assigned different roles?
They develop an understanding of the importance of organizational structure and learn that each member's focus on their role is vital to the group's success.
3. What ability is improved when the team identifies and solves key problems together?
It enhances members' critical thinking skills.
4. What is the aim of focusing on interpersonal relationships in team building?
To improve members' cooperation abilities such as giving support, accepting help, and communicating and sharing.
5. What kind of team usually functions more effectively?
Teams with fewer interpersonal conflicts usually function more effectively; a facilitator helps build mutual trust and open communication.

任务3 中餐服务的形式与特点

Task 3: The Forms and Characteristics of Chinese F&B Service
案例导入Case Introduction
In ancient times, at a banquet such as the one where Xiang Zhuang performed a sword dance, each person had a separate table and sat in different directions — this was the separate dining system (fen can zhi). Even Xiang Zhuang had to pass Xiang Yu to reach Liu Bang, so the configuration made assassination difficult. This shows that at that time everyone had their own table, seated in different directions, practicing separate dining. Nowadays, the Chinese custom of eating the reunion dinner (nian ye fan) on New Year's Eve has gradually formed. On this most important annual festival, people across the country, whether eating dumplings or rice cakes, sit together around one table with all the dishes placed on it, sharing a happy reunion dinner. Thinking and discussion: The change of dining styles was formed by the intertwined influence of geographical, cultural and political factors in different periods. So, are there other forms of modern Chinese food service?
在古代，如项庄舞剑的宴席上，每人一张食案、分坐不同方向，实行分餐制。项庄要越过范增才能刺向刘邦，说明当时每人一张食案、分坐于不同方向。在他们之间有一片充足的空间。 然而现在，中国人全家人吃年夜饭的习俗渐渐形成，在每年的除夕，这个一年一度的中国人最重要的节日中，大江南北的人们，无论是吃饺子还是年糕，都会聚团团围坐在一起，菜肴全部放在一个桌上，全家人共同吃上一顿其乐融融的年夜饭。 思考讨论：进餐方式的变化是根据不同时期的地理、文化、政治因素的相互交错影响而形成的。那么，现代中餐服务还有其他的形式吗？
知识准备Knowledge Preparation

The Forms of Chinese F&B Service

In its long-term development, Chinese food has gradually formed certain service methods adapted to the characteristics of dishes. With the diversification of dining needs, Chinese F&B service has also undergone change, and new methods have appeared. At present, the common service forms are as follows:

(1) Shared meal service — This is a common service method in team meals, conference meals and informal banquets. Instead of using their own chopsticks, each person uses public chopsticks and a public spoon, or the host serves guests with communal tableware to show hospitality. Its advantage is that guests have more freedom; the host can serve others or guests take what they need, so the atmosphere is harmonious. Fewer service personnel are needed and the technical requirement is not high. However, guests get less service, and since all dishes are served together, the table easily becomes messy in the second half of the meal.

(2) Turntable service — A common mode in Chinese service, applicable to large round tables over 1.5 m in diameter, for both casual and banquet service. A 1.5 m table is equipped with a 0.8–0.9 m turntable; a 1.8 m table with a 1.0–1.2 m turntable. Each seat should be at least 0.3–0.4 m from the turntable edge. It suits group and upscale banquets; guests serve themselves or are served by the waiter. Traditional carving/portioning is also a performance but requires highly skilled staff and can dirty the turntable, especially with soups and juicy dishes.

(3) Individual dining service — A popular mode developed in recent years, absorbing the advantages of western service combined with Chinese service, called "eating Chinese food in the western way", suitable for formal high-end banquets. It is divided into "side table service" (a fixed or movable side table with clean plates for portioning) and "distributing food service" (dishes on a small round tray, held in the left hand, served with spoon and fork from the guest's left, starting with the guest of honor and host, then clockwise). It gives more service, disturbs guests less, is hygienic and fits modern habits, but costs more labor and requires highly skilled waiters.

The Characteristics of Chinese F&B Service

(1) Modern Chinese F&B service begins using the tray — The skill of using trays in western service was the first aspect to influence Chinese service; today, whether in international hotels or ordinary restaurants, waiters all use trays.
(2) The use of napkins changes the dining etiquette — Napkins are imported; traditional Chinese service did not offer them. Western napkin use is elegant etiquette. Western service popularized court/aristocratic dining culture, while Chinese continued popular social dining. Napkin use changed Chinese dining civility; Chinese tend to tuck napkins under the plate — a Chinese feature.
(3) The reprogramming of the serving procedure — Traditional Chinese service had no clear sequence; modern service follows cold dish, soup, hot dish, dessert — borrowed from the west, influencing both culture and dietary science.
(4) The combination of service forms — Modern Chinese service thoroughly absorbed British, American and French forms. There are single English (family/"wei can"), single American (plate/"ge ke"), and single French (cart/"tang zuo") services, as well as banquet service combining all three — unusual in modern western service.
(5) The usage of split fork and turntable — Split forks were also introduced from the west, the best example of accepting different cultures; the turntable, originally for convenience, has become a feature of Chinese service and table setting.

一、中餐服务的形式

中餐在长期发展过程中，逐步形成了一定的服务方式，并使之与菜肴特点相适应。随着人们就餐方式的多样化，中餐服务方式也经历了变革，出现了许多新的方式。目前常见的有：

（一）共餐式服务：目前团队餐、会议餐及非正式宴请中常用的服务方式。现有方式改变了传统做法，不再各人自己用筷子夹菜，而是用公筷、公勺，或由主人用公用餐具为客人夹菜，以示热情好客。优点是客人用餐自由度大，主人可给客人夹菜或客人各取所需，气氛融洽；所需服务人员少，一个服务员可同时服务多桌；对技术要求不高。但客人得到的服务较少；所有菜肴一起上，用餐后半程易出现杯盘狼藉的现象。

（二）转盘式服务：中餐中较普遍的服务方式，适用于直径1.5m以上的大圆桌，可用于便餐和宴会。转盘配置有讲究：直径1.5m的圆桌配0.8–0.9m转盘，1.8m的圆桌配1.0–1.2m转盘；每个餐座至少离转盘边0.3–0.4m。适合团体就餐和高档宴请；客人可自取或由服务员派菜；传统分菜服务也是表演，但要求服务员技术熟练、分菜均匀、份量一致，且易弄脏转盘，尤其分带汤汁多的菜肴时。

（三）分餐式服务：近年发展起来的流行服务方式，吸收西餐优点并与中餐结合，称为"中餐西吃"，适用于较正规的高档宴会。分为"旁桌服务"（宴会桌旁摆固定或活动服务桌，放洁净餐盘备用）和"派菜服务"（菜肴放小圆托盘，左手托盘，右手用服务勺、叉分菜；次序为主宾、主人，再顺时针；一律从客人左侧分菜，避免托盘与勺叉交错）。适用于中式宴会，提供更多服务、少干扰客人谈话，较卫生，适合现代消费习惯；但费人工、对操作技能要求高。

二、中餐服务的特点

（一）现代中餐服务开始使用托盘：西餐使用托盘的技巧最先影响中餐；现在无论国际大酒店中餐厅还是一般餐饮场所，服务员无不使用托盘。
（二）餐巾的使用改变用餐礼仪：餐巾是舶来品，传统中餐不提供；西餐餐巾轻擦嘴角是一种优雅礼仪。西餐普及宫廷贵族餐饮文化，中餐因贵族文化断层延续大众化社会餐饮。餐巾改变了用餐文明礼仪，但中餐中人们更愿意将纸巾压在餐碟底下，这是中餐一大特色。
（三）上菜程序的重新规划：传统中餐对上菜程序无明确规定；现代中餐按冷菜、汤、热菜到甜品的顺序，套用西餐顺序，影响及于饮食科学。
（四）各种服务形式的结合：现代中餐彻底吸收英、美、法三种经典西餐服务形式并融合进传统饮食文化，既有单一英式（围餐）、单一美式（位上/各客）、单一法式（堂做），也有综合三者的宴会服务，这在现代西餐中颇为少见。
（五）分餐叉和转盘的使用：分餐叉同样从西餐传入，是接纳不同民族文化最好的例子；转盘本为方便取菜，如今已成为中餐服务及摆台特色。

任务实施Task Implementation

Step 1: Form a study group of 6–8 people. Each group goes to a hotel Chinese restaurant to observe the service process and understand the service mode and characteristics.

Step 2: Group representatives share observation results in class; the teacher comments, analyses, introduces the section and arranges a simulation exercise.

Step 3: Arrange the catering training room into a restaurant with two types of tables (standard round tables for 10, one with a turntable and one without). Prepare plates of various sizes (students bring their own food/snacks to simulate dishes). Each group takes turns acting as guests and waiters, practicing shared meal, turntable and individual dining services.

第一步：成立学习小组，每组6—8人，各组深入酒店中餐厅，观摩中餐厅服务流程，了解中餐厅服务的方式和特点。

第二步：各小组代表课堂上分享观摩结果，教师点评、分析并引入本节内容，讲授本节内容并安排课堂模拟演练。

第三步：将餐饮实训室布置成拥有两种规格餐桌（10人标准圆桌）的餐厅：一种无转盘，另一种有转盘。提前准备好各种规格的餐盘（由学生自带食品或零食放在盘中模拟菜肴）。根据本节内容，各小组轮流扮演客人和中餐厅服务员，进行共餐式服务、转盘式服务、分餐式服务三种服务的模拟练习。

任务评价Task Evaluation

评价指标Evaluation Item要求Requirement
操作规范，流程完整、顺畅The service conforms to the specification; the process is complete and smooth分餐式服务操作规范、流程顺畅Individual dining service is standard and smooth.

拓展阅读Extended Reading

Chinese Dining Etiquette

Eating is ordinary yet very important. Especially in China, known as the "Kingdom of Cuisine", improper behavior at the table is a breach of etiquette.

Chinese dining etiquette is mainly reflected in seating arrangement, dining style, environment and the selection of dishes. At banquets or feasts, the seating order is taken seriously; it varies with the purpose and nature of the banquet. There are no strict rules — it follows custom, highlighting the guest of honor while reasonably arranging others to create a happy, harmonious atmosphere. China has traditionally "respected the left" and "honored the east". Modern banquets usually use round tables, with the seat facing the main door being the most honorable. The guest of honor and host's spouse should be seated first, to show respect.

Chinese mostly use chopsticks, giving rise to many rules and taboos — for example, one should not gesture with chopsticks or gobble food. Generally, Chinese diners value conversation with one another.

In the past, Chinese paid little attention to the external environment, only caring for a lively atmosphere. Now they tend to choose restaurants with elegant environments, rich varieties of dishes and high-quality service.

There are few rules about ordering; usually the elder, ladies or guests order first. When it is the host's turn, they should consider the guests' preferences.

1. In what aspects is Chinese dining etiquette mainly reflected?
In seating arrangement, dining style, environment, and the selection of dishes.
2. Which direction and position are traditionally most honorable at a Chinese banquet?
China traditionally "respected the left" and "honored the east"; at modern banquets, the seat facing the main door is the most honorable.
3. Who should be seated first at a banquet?
The guest of honor and the host's spouse should be arranged first, to show respect.
4. What do Chinese diners value most when eating together?
They place more emphasis on communication and conversation with one another.
5. How has the choice of dining environment changed for Chinese people?
In the past they cared little for the environment, only wanting liveliness; now they prefer restaurants with elegant environments, varied dishes and high-quality service.

任务4 西餐服务

Task 4: Western F&B Service
案例导入Case Introduction
It is said that one summer Li Hongzhang went abroad on a visit. After arriving, a foreign minister invited him to dine at the embassy. According to that country's custom, an appetizer soup is served before the meal and dessert after. After the meal, cups of steaming white stuff were served. Not knowing it was ice cream, Li took a puff out of habit and blew on it, then put it in his mouth and found it cold. The foreigners around laughed. Li knew he was being laughed at, but calmly said: "Thank you for your generous hospitality. I will return the favor and invite you all someday." The next day, the foreigners came to the banquet Li had prepared. According to foreign custom, an aperitif was served first, with something on top, and the guests were so spicy they shouted and could not speak. Thinking and discussion: Western dining habits differ from country to country. What are the differences in food, drink and service methods?
据传有一年夏天，大臣李鸿章出国访问。到了国外后，外国使臣请李鸿章到大使馆就餐。按照该国的饮食习惯，餐前喝开胃汤，餐后吃甜品。饭后，一杯杯冒着白色热气的奶制品被端了上来，李鸿章不知道这是冰激凌，吸了一口习惯性地用嘴吹了一阵子，放到嘴里才发现是冰的。身边的洋人哈哈大笑，李鸿章知道自己被嘲笑了，不动声色地对洋人说："感谢贵国的厚谊款待，改天我也回请各位大人。" 第二天，洋人应邀来到李鸿章设宴的地方。按照洋人的惯例，先上开胃酒，汤上面是……人被辣得哇哇大叫，说不出话来。 思考讨论：西方各个国家的餐饮习惯不同，饮食及服务方式又有哪些区别呢？
知识准备Knowledge Preparation

Western food service mainly includes French service, American service, British service, Russian service and Buffet service. These five kinds differ from country to country and influence each other through the times. There is no single best mode; each is designed to satisfy customers' needs and meet different conditions.

1. French service — Restaurants use mainly silverware; two trained service personnel cook in front of guests on a cart or service table and serve the cooked food on hot plates. Basic principles: (1) Dishes leave the kitchen on silverware and are reheated on service tables or carts. (2) The server holds fork and spoon and takes food as the guest requires from the silverware. (3) When plated, the assistant waiter presents it from the guest's right. (4) Except bread, butter, salad and special dishes, all service is from the guest's right. (5) Clear-up is also from the right. Another feature is the finger bowl, provided with any hand-eaten dish.

2. American service — The most common western form, also called plate service; the simplest, fastest and lowest-cost. All dishes are plated in the kitchen and served directly. Besides the main course, bread, salad and coffee/drinks are served. Principles: (1) Dishes from the left, drinks from the right. (2) All dishes cleared from the right. (3) A skilled waiter serves 3–4 guests at a time.

3. British service — Originates from British tradition where the host cuts, portions and serves; in restaurants, staff play the host. Principles: (1) Staff take the silver platter from the kitchen and serve clockwise from the right. (2) Show the dish to the guest with the left hand. (3) Use fork and spoon to serve from the guest's left. Similar to French, but guests cannot serve themselves; service starts counterclockwise from the female guest of honor on her left, so it is also called "silver plate service".

4. Russian service — Originated in Russia, first seen in Europe during the Napoleonic Wars; became the most popular palace service for its simplicity and speed. Principles: (1) Before serving, the waiter passes plates clockwise from the guest's right — cold dish with cold plate, hot dish with heated plate. (2) Carry the large silver platter to the table, stand on the guest's left, serve counterclockwise from the left with the right hand. (3) Drinks and clear-up are on the right.

5. Buffet service — Guests choose food freely; there is no fixed menu. It is divided into:
(1) Swedish buffet service (European buffet) — Priced per guest; offers soups, salads, meats, desserts, fruits and drinks. Except large meats cut by the chef, guests serve themselves. Staff must watch food levels, heating/insulation equipment, and clear empty plates.
(2) Luncheonette buffet service — Guests walk along the display; staff serve chosen food and check out at the exit; guests carry meals to the dining area. Unlike the Swedish type, guests usually clear their own plates; common in school dining rooms.

西餐服务主要有5种：法式服务、美式服务、英式服务、俄式服务和自助式服务。这5种因起源国别不同而有所差异，在时代变迁中相互影响。没有哪一种服务方式是最好的，每一种的存在都是为了迎合客人的需求及应对不同状况的要求。

一、法式服务：餐厅所用餐具以银器为主，两名受训服务人员在手推车或服务桌前现场烹饪，再将烹制好的食物分盛于热餐盘服侍客人。基本原则：(1) 菜肴从厨房用银器盛出，在服务桌或手推车上重新加热。(2) 服务员左手拿服务叉和勺，按客人所需从银器中取菜。(3) 餐盘上好后，由助理服务员从客人右侧端送。(4) 除面包、黄油、沙拉及特殊菜肴外，所有服务均在客人右侧进行。(5) 餐后清理也在客人右侧。另一特点是提供洗手盅，凡需用手取食的菜肴都同时提供洗手盅。

二、美式服务：西餐厅最常见的形式，又称餐盘式服务，最简单便捷、快速且成本低廉。所有菜肴在上菜前已在厨房盛妥，直接端到餐桌；除主菜外还有面包、沙拉及咖啡饮品。原则：(1) 左侧上菜，右侧上饮品。(2) 所有菜肴在客人右侧清理。(3) 一名熟练服务员一次可同时服务3—4位客人。

三、英式服务：源自英国传统饮食习惯，由主人切割、配量、分菜；餐厅中由服务人员完成主人角色。原则：(1) 服务人员将银盘从厨房取出，顺时针在客人右侧上菜。(2) 用左手先向客人展示菜品。(3) 用服务叉和勺从客人左侧分菜。与法式类似，唯一不同是法式客人可自取，英式由服务人员从女主宾左侧逆时针分菜，故又称"银盘服务"。

四、俄式服务：起源于俄罗斯，拿破仑战争时初次出现在欧洲大陆，因简单快速成为皇宫最受欢迎的服务方式。原则：(1) 上菜前服务员按客人右手方向顺时针递送餐盘，冷菜用冷盘、热菜用温过的餐盘。(2) 从厨房将装好菜肴的大银盘托到餐桌旁，站立客人左侧，右手从左侧逆时针分菜。(3) 服务酒水和撤盘都在客人右侧操作。

五、自助式服务：最大特点是客人随意挑选喜爱的食物，且餐厅没有固定菜单。分为：(1) 瑞典式自助餐（欧式自助餐）：以人数计价，供应汤、沙拉、肉类、甜点、水果及饮品；除大块肉类由厨师切割分派外，其余客人自取；服务人员须注意食物数量、加热保温设备及清理空盘。(2) 简便式自助餐：客人沿取餐区行走，服务人员提供所选食物并在出口结账，客人自行端到用餐区；餐盘通常由客人自行清理，常见于学校餐厅。

Step 2: Form a study group of 5 students; one acts as the restaurant attendant, the other four as guests. Take turns to simulate French, British, American and Russian service (the 5 students rotate roles).

Step 3: Each group randomly selects a western F&B service to be evaluated and conducts the simulation test.

拓展阅读Extended Reading

The Development History of Western Food

Western food generally refers to dishes developed according to the dietary habits of Western countries. In the history of western cooking, the earliest cuisine with both written records and physical evidence appeared in ancient Egypt; a kitchen and dining room were found in city ruins around 2000 BC. The Roman Empire was influenced by Egyptian and Greek cultures; chefs' skills and social status improved, promoting the development of western cuisine.

After a long period, in 1533, when King Henry II of France married Catherine de' Medici of Italy — who loved the culinary arts — the French integrated traditional French cuisine with Italian cuisine, founded a cooking school and created a new era of French cuisine. Therefore, western cuisine and service are represented by the French style.

At the end of the 17th century, French cuisine was famous worldwide. The cooking competitions held by King Louis XIV promoted the French culinary industry, and many famous chefs emerged.

An important feature of modern western cuisine is its growing global influence, due to the development of the New World and British colonial expansion. By the 19th century, the dish characteristics and dietary styles of European countries had basically taken shape.

Western food was introduced to China after the Opium War in 1840. Imperialist countries swarmed in and western dining customs followed, forming a new industry. Chinese-owned "western food restaurants" (then called "xi can guan") appeared, along with cafes and bakeries. The earliest was "Yipinxiang" on Fuzhou Road in Shanghai; Beijing's western food industry began in the Guangxu period, and in 1903 the French created "Deli Bakery". After the Third Plenary Session of the 11th Central Committee, the opening-up policy and rapid tourism growth led to many star-rated foreign-related hotels hiring foreign chefs and sending Chinese chefs abroad to study, giving new development to China's western food industry.

1. Where and when did the earliest western cuisine with written and physical evidence appear?
In ancient Egypt, around 2000 BC.
2. Who integrated French and Italian cooking to create a new era of French cuisine?
After Henry II of France married Catherine de' Medici of Italy in 1533, the French integrated the two cuisines and founded a cooking school.
3. What promoted the French culinary industry at the end of the 17th century?
The cooking competitions frequently held by King Louis XIV.
4. When was western food introduced to China?
After the Opium War in 1840.
5. What accelerated the new development of China's western food industry after reform and opening-up?
The opening-up policy and rapid tourism growth brought many foreign-related hotels hiring foreign chefs and sending Chinese chefs abroad to study.

项目二 预订、问候及领位服务

Project 2 Reservation, Greeting and Hostess Service
项目概述Project Overview
As a restaurant operator, you would hope the restaurant is full every day, with guests seated on a first-come, first-served basis and no advance reservation needed. Or you might hope guests would thoughtfully call ahead to ask when it is convenient to come, so as not to put pressure and burden on the chef and service staff. But this is only a wishful thought. In reality, almost every guest wants to dine between 11:30–12:30 at noon and 6:00–7:00 in the evening. Therefore, the restaurant must solve the problem of reservations.
作为餐厅经营者，希望餐厅每天都能客满，客人先到先坐，无须提前预订。或者客人在用餐前会体贴地给餐厅打电话，询问什么时候方便前来用餐，以免给厨师和服务人员造成压力和负担。但是，这只能是设想，现实中几乎所有的客人都想在上午11:30–12:30、晚上6:00–7:00之间用餐。因此，餐厅必须解决预订的问题。
At present, many restaurants take reservations by telephone, and of course some are made by guests coming to the restaurant in person. Whether by phone or in person, this is an opportunity for the restaurant to provide first-class service. As a restaurant operator, you must ensure that every service staff member who may be asked to take a reservation is professionally trained — not only proficient in using the food and beverage reservation system, but also possessing proper language communication and telephone etiquette.
目前，许多餐厅的预订是通过电话来完成的，当然也有部分预订是客人亲自到餐厅完成的。无论是电话还是当面完成的预订，这都是餐厅为客人提供一流服务的机会。作为餐厅经营者，要确保每一个可能被要求预订的服务人员都经过专业培训，不仅表现在熟练使用餐饮预订系统，而且具有恰当的语言沟通和电话礼仪。
When guests come to dine, apart from enjoying delicious dishes and a relaxed, pleasant dining environment, they also have great expectations of the service provided by the staff. As a guest walks to the restaurant door, a warm greeting from the service staff will quickly close the distance with the guest. The staff can greet guests in different ways; the most common is "Good morning / Good afternoon / Good evening, Sir / Madam, welcome to XX Restaurant." If the guest is a regular, the first greeting should include the guest's surname.
客人到餐厅用餐，除了享受美味的菜肴、轻松惬意的用餐环境，对服务人员提供的服务也抱有极大的期望。客人走到餐厅门口，服务人员一声亲切的问候会迅速拉近与客人的距离。服务人员可以用不同的方式来问候客人，最常见的便是"早上好/中午好/晚上好，先生/女士，欢迎光临XX餐厅。"如果是比较热熟的客人，服务人员的第一声问候应该是包含客人姓氏的。
After the greeting, having asked whether the guest has a reservation, the service staff smiles and leads the guest to the seat. During the seating process, the service staff should keep communicating with the guest. What seems to be accomplished in a few minutes in fact reflects the precise and proper service flow of the restaurant staff, which lets the guest experience a home-away-from-home service before even starting the meal.
问候完毕，询问客人是否有预订后，服务人员微笑着将客人带到餐位上。在领位的过程中，服务人员要保持与客人的交流。看似在几分钟内完成的动作，实则体现了餐厅服务人员精准到位的服务流程，这将会使客人还未开始用餐，就已经体验到餐厅宾至如归的服务。

任务1 餐饮预订认知

Task 1: A Cognition of Reservation

知识准备Knowledge Preparation

The Concept of Reservation

Reservation is a kind of service commitment to the guests who book meals, ensuring that the table can be reserved for the guests at the appointed time. Reservation is usually the first contact between the guests and the restaurant. The attitude of the reservation staff directly affects the guests' first impression of the restaurant. Every guest wants courteous, warm and efficient service. If guests are not satisfied during the reservation process, they are likely to choose another restaurant.

The Way of Reservation

At present, the way of restaurant reservation mainly includes telephone reservation and walk-in reservation.

The Contents of Reservation

The contents of reservation generally include the name of the reservation person, telephone number, number of diners, arrival time and other special requirements of the guest.

Reservation System

Currently, TMS (Table Management System) and ResPAK (Reservation PAK) are most popular in hotels' food and beverage reservation systems.

Procedures for Making a Reservation

Greeting — getting guests' reservation information — making a reservation — recording guests' special requirements — confirming reservation information.

预订的概念

预订是对订餐客人的一种服务承诺，确保能够为客人在约定的时间保留餐位。预订通常是客人与餐厅的第一次接触，预订人员的态度、用语直接影响客人对餐厅的第一印象。每个客人都希望得到礼貌、热情和高效的服务。如果在预订过程中客人不满意，他们很可能会选择其他餐厅。

预订的方式

目前餐饮预订的方式主要有电话预订与当面预订两种。

预订的内容

预订的内容一般包括预订人的姓名、预订人的电话、就餐人数、抵达时间以及客人的特殊要求等。

预订系统

目前，酒店餐饮预订系统主要使用TMS 和 ResPAK。

四、受理预订的程序

问候客人—聆听客人预订需求—接受预订—记录客人特殊需求—确认预订信息。

Step 3: The instructor introduces the steps of making a reservation.

拓展阅读Extended Reading

Introduction to the Food and Beverage Reservation Management System

How to arrange restaurant seats reasonably so as to maximize profit is a problem that every restaurant operator cares about most and urgently wishes to solve. Using TMS (Table Management System) and ResPAK (Reservation PAK) management systems can not only help the restaurant manage the operation of seats, but also provide a series of analytical reports. The food and beverage reservation management system adopts flexible system modules and a common hardware platform, which effectively improves the reservation efficiency of the restaurant, raises customer satisfaction, and invisibly brings more profit to the restaurant.

The functional features of the food and beverage reservation management system include:

1. Accurate reservation processing. After a restaurant adopts a reservation management system, all customer call-in reservations and all reservation information are processed centrally, avoiding errors and duplicate bookings caused by manual handling, thus ensuring the accuracy of seat reservations while improving efficiency. The system can realize interactive reservation; the reservation center can support 24-hour telephone reservation, and the uninterrupted telephone reservation service is especially suitable for the reservation needs of all kinds of restaurants.

2. Effective waiting management. The reservation management system can effectively handle both walk-in customers' seat management and call-in reservation customers. For walk-in customers, the operator can quickly browse seat usage and arrange seats promptly. For call-in reservation customers, seats are arranged in time; when the restaurant is fully booked, the customer can be added to a waiting list. The waiting management of customers helps shorten their queuing time.

3. Sharing of customer data. Through interfaces with other systems (hotel system, Gourmate, iGourmate, etc.), customer data is shared. Just by browsing the customer profile on the reservation system, the operator can immediately know the customer's personal information and arrange seats according to his or her preferences.

1. What are the two main reservation management systems mentioned in the passage?
TMS (Table Management System) and ResPAK (Reservation PAK).
2. What are the three functional features of the F&B reservation management system described?
Accurate reservation processing, effective waiting management, and sharing of customer data.
3. How does the system ensure accuracy in reservation processing?
All incoming reservation calls and information are processed centrally, avoiding errors and duplicate bookings caused by manual handling; it also supports 24-hour interactive telephone reservation.
4. How does the waiting management feature help walk-in and call-in customers?
The operator can quickly browse seat usage and arrange seats promptly; for fully booked periods, customers are added to a waiting list, which shortens queuing time.
5. How is customer data shared with other systems?
Through interfaces with other systems (hotel system, Gourmate, iGourmate, etc.), the operator can view customer profiles and arrange seats according to their preferences.

任务2 预订服务流程

Task 2: Reservation Service Process
案例导入Case Introduction
A restaurant in Hangzhou offers a Chinese restaurant where, instead of charging a service fee, the average cost per person is only about 150 yuan for a very satisfying meal — quite cost-effective compared with other hotels. Since opening, many guests book both their next meal time and seats right after dining. The executive chef believes the booking boom is also due to the restaurant not accepting a second-round ("turnover") reservation. "To guarantee the quality of guests' dining, we do not accept the second-round booking." Some guests even post on social media. Restaurants such as Chuanwei Guan, Mingren Mingjia, Hangzhou Jiujia and Weizhuang are equally busy. The operation manager of Chuanwei Guan said: "In the three days before National Day, the private rooms were basically fully booked, only the hall had vacancies. To improve service quality, we moved the latest arrival time for accepted reservations from 6 p.m. to 5 p.m." In the past, popular restaurants often had guests queuing at the door while private rooms sat empty, and many guests even complained. To enhance the restaurant's brand image, avoid giving a sense of hunger marketing, and let more guests eat earlier, many restaurants shifted their reservation time half an hour earlier. But restaurants such as Xinduzuo, Waiwa Jia and Lao Tour Youbao, which do not accept reservations, will require guests to go early to take a number during National Day. Thinking and discussion: In the digital age, how can reservations be made efficient, fast and intelligent?
杭州城中香格里拉大酒店中餐厅不仅不收服务费，而且人均只要150元就能吃得很满意，相对于其他酒店，性价比很高。自开业以后，不少客人用餐后就会顺带把之后的用餐时间和座位都预订了。中餐厅厨师长俞师傅认为，预订火爆还归结于餐厅不接受二轮就餐预订，就是俗称的"翻台"。"为了保证客人用餐的品质，二轮我们不订。"热门餐厅以前经常发生客人在店门口排长队，包厢大桌子却还是空着的情况，不少客人甚至会投诉。为了提升餐厅的整体品牌，不给客人带来一种饥饿营销的感觉，也让更多的客人可以尽早吃上饭，不少餐厅都把预订时间往前挪了半个小时。川味观、名人名家、杭州酒家、味庄这些餐厅的预订情况也同样火爆。但新都座、外玖家、老头儿油爆虾这些不接受预订的餐厅，在国庆期间想吃上估计就要花上点儿时间早点去取号了。思考讨论：数字时代，预定如何实现高效、快捷、智能化？
知识准备Knowledge Preparation

Reservation Service Process

The Process Form of Reservation Service (R22):

  1. Answer the phone within three rings; proactively greet the guest politely and accurately state the restaurant's name and your own name.
  2. Express willingness to provide services to the guest promptly, such as "What can I do for you?" "I am glad to help you", etc.
  3. When you know the guest is calling to book a meal, proactively introduce yourself and show willingness to serve. (For walk-in reservation: if there is a reservation clerk in the restaurant, the server needs to lead the guest to the clerk and make the reservation.)
  4. Ask the guest's name politely and then call his or her last name to show respect.
  5. Investigate the requirements: listen to the guest's introduction to learn the dining situation, date and time, number of guests, table number and other special requirements.
  6. After obtaining agreement, arrange the appropriate tables or rooms for the guest and inform them.
  7. Repeat the reservation information and let the guest confirm.
  8. Leave the guest's name, telephone number and other contact information.
  9. Let the guest know the reservation deadline (last time the table is held).
  10. If the reservation is already full, let the guest know and apologize.

Reservation Record

  • Fill in the reservation form.
  • If the menu is already set, immediately notify the restaurant manager and the chef.
  • If the reservation does not include a menu, just notify the front desk.
  • If the reservation has special requirements, promptly notify the headwaiter and the chef.
  • Record the reservation details in the reservation notebook or enter them into the reservation system. A single restaurant's reservation notebook is normally one per month and is archived after use.

Service Operation Announcements

(1) Reservation registration elements. (a) Basic information of the guest: name of the guest or the team, contact number and address, number of guests, dinner time, purpose (conference or travel package meal), etc. (b) Guests' requirements: the type and number of seats or private rooms required; price standard; other special requirements, such as restaurant layout and dish requirements, etc.

(2) Description of restaurant policy. Discounts and payment methods; reservation holding time; policy for guests changing or cancelling the reservation.

(3) Tracking the reservation. The more important or longer-interval reservations should be tracked. Tracking means actively contacting guests to confirm or change the reservation and trying to detect possible accidents as early as possible. Group reservations in particular need to be reconfirmed.

(4) Reserving seats for the guests. Reservation is a commitment to the guests, so seats must be reserved within the appointed time. In actual reception, guests often fail to arrive at the appointed time or do not come at all. Therefore, when reserving seats, stress the importance of time, voluntarily tell the guest the time limit for holding the seat, and seats beyond the deadline will be given to other guests.

(5) Changing the reservation. If the restaurant needs to change the time or place of a reservation for special reasons, obtain the guest's consent in advance. The standard and conditions should be favorable and meet the guest's requirements.

(6) Improving reservation service. The reservation clerk must be proficient in booking business and possess good service quality and moral awareness. The service should be proactive, show a good service attitude and try to meet guests' needs. Avoid answering the phone late, failing to use polite expressions, not further confirming the reservation, and not recording specific requirements in detail. If the guest's request cannot be met, do not reject it outright without offering an alternative suggestion.

预订服务流程

预订服务流程表（R22）：

  1. 三声内接听电话；主动礼貌向客人问好，并准确报出餐厅名称及自己姓名。
  2. 及时表示愿意为客人提供服务，如"有什么事情可以帮您？""我很乐意为您服务"等。
  3. 知道客人是来订餐时，要主动向客人介绍自己，并表示愿意为客人服务。（当面预订服务：如餐厅有预订员，服务员需引领客人找预订员办理预订。）
  4. 礼貌地询问客人姓名，然后称呼其姓氏以表示尊重。
  5. 了解需求—及其他要求：聆听客人的介绍，了解就餐状况、用餐日期和时间、就餐人数、台号及其他特殊要求。
  6. 在得到同意后，为客人安排合适的餐位或包间，并告知客人。
  7. 复述客人预订的内容，并请客人确认。
  8. 留下客人的电话、姓名及其他联系方式。
  9. 告知客人预订餐位的最后保留时间。
  10. 如预订已满，告知客人并致歉。

记录

  • 填好预订表。
  • 已订菜单的预订立即通知餐厅经理和厨师长。
  • 未订菜单的预订，只通知前台即可。
  • 有特殊要求的预订，要及时通知餐厅总领班和厨师长。
  • 将预订的详细内容记录在预订登记本上或录入预订系统。餐厅预订登记本一般每月一本，用后存档。

服务操作注意事项

（一）预订登记要素。（1）客人基本信息：客人姓名或团队名称、联系电话、联系地址；就餐人数、就餐时间、就餐目的（会议或旅游包餐）等。（2）客人要求：所需餐位或包房的种类及数量、价格标准；其他特殊要求，如餐厅布置、菜品要求等。

（二）餐厅政策说明。折扣优惠及付款方式；餐位保留时间，对客人的预订变更、取消预订的政策规定。

（三）跟踪预订。较重要的、时间较远的预订都要跟踪。跟踪是指要主动联系客人，确认预订或者更改预订，争取尽早发现可能发生的意外。对大型的预订尤其需要再次进行确认。

（四）为客人保留餐位。预订是对订餐客人的一种承诺，因此在约定时间内必须为客人保留餐位。在实际接待服务中，常出现客人预订后未按约定时间到达，或不来就餐的现象。所以，在为客人预订餐位时，应强调时间的重要性，主动告诉客人为其保留座位的时间期限，超过保留期限的餐位会让给其他客人使用。

（五）更改客人预订。餐厅如有特殊情况需要更改客人预订的时间和地点，要事先征得客人同意。标准和条件应对客人有利，并达到客人的要求。

（六）提升预订服务质量。预订人员既要精通预订业务，又要具备良好的服务素质和道德意识。预订服务应注意服务的主动性，展现良好的服务态度，尽量满足客人的需求。避免接听电话不及时、不使用礼貌用语、对客人的预订没有进一步确认、对客人的具体要求不做详细记录等情况发生。如果无法满足客人要求，不可没有提出替代性建议便立即回绝。

任务实施Task Implementation

Step 1: The teacher sets the scenario: Ms. Lucy wants to reserve a table for 4 people at 7:00 p.m. in a quieter area, but the reservation clerk Emma checks the system and finds only 8:30 p.m. is available. Suppose you are the reservation clerk of this restaurant — how would you make a reservation for Ms. Lucy? Please consider various outcomes and make the reservation for Ms. Lucy in both Chinese and English.

Step 2: The teacher lists the relevant English vocabulary, phrases and sentence patterns for students' reference.

  • — Where do you prefer to sit?（您更喜欢坐在哪里？）
  • — A quiet area.（安静的区域。）

Step 3: The teacher gives a demonstration case and sets an example.

Emma: Good morning, Yuexi Restaurant. This is Emma speaking. How may I help you?
Lucy: I'd like to reserve a table for four at 7:00 p.m. Could you please arrange it for us?
Emma: Just a moment, let me see if there are any suitable seats available. I'm very sorry, Ms., there are no available seats at 7:00 p.m., but we do have some seats available at 8:30 p.m. Would you like to make a reservation?
Lucy: Let me see. It's a little late.
Emma: Yes, but usually, the restaurant is very quiet at that time.
Lucy: That's true. In fact, I really need a quiet place.
Emma: If so, I'd like to recommend the Prince's Room, which is a quiet and comfortable private room. And free juice will be provided after 8:30 p.m.
Lucy: Sounds great. I'll make a reservation at 8:30 p.m.
Emma: All right, Ms. Let me confirm again. There will be four people in the Prince's Room for dinner at 8:30 p.m. this evening. Excuse me, may I know how to address you?
Lucy: I'm Lucy.
Emma: Thanks a lot, Ms. Lucy. Goodbye.

Step 4: Take the group (5 people per group) as a unit, in which one student plays the reservation clerk and the other 4 students act as the guests. Simulate both Chinese and English telephone reservation and walk-in reservation (5 students take turns to simulate).

第一步：教师导入本节情景设定，露西女士想预订晚上7点在较安静的区域就餐，共4人，但预订员埃玛查阅后发现只有晚上8点半才有位置。假设你是这个餐厅的预订员，你会怎样为露西女士做预订？请考虑多种结果，并使用中英双语为露西女士做预订。

第二步：教师列出餐饮预订用到的相关英文词汇、短语或句型，以供学生参考。

  • — 您更喜欢坐在哪里？
  • — 安静的区域。

第三步：教师给出示范案例，并予以示范。

埃玛：早上好，越溪餐厅。我是埃玛。请问有什么可以帮您？
露西：我想预订晚上7点4个人的餐位，可以帮我们安排一下吗？
埃玛：请稍等，我先查看一下是否有合适的位子。（过了一会儿……）十分抱歉，女士，7点没有合适的位子了，8点30分有空位，您看需要帮您预订吗？
露西：让我想想，时间有点晚了。
埃玛：女士，通常那个时间餐厅会十分的安静。
露西：这倒是，事实上我的确需要一个安静的地方。
埃玛：如果可以，我给您推荐王子包间。那是一个安静且空间舒适的包间，餐厅在8点30分后会提供免费的果汁。
露西：听起来很不错，我预订8点30分的吧。
埃玛：好的，女士。再帮您确认一下，今晚8点30分王子包间4人用餐。请问，我可以知道如何称呼您吗？
露西：我是露西。
埃玛：十分感谢，露西女士。再见！

第四步：以小组（5人1组）为单位，其中1位学生扮演预订员，另外4位学生扮演订餐客人，分别模拟进行中英文电话预订和当面预订（5位学生轮流进行情景模拟）。

拓展阅读Extended Reading

Common Expressions for Restaurant Reservation Abroad

When you make a reservation at a restaurant abroad, the server who answers will first introduce his or her name and the restaurant name, and then politely ask if you need help. As a guest, do not be intimidated by the server's fast speech, or worry that you did not catch whether the name was Susan or Mary — just say hello and then state your purpose.

Chinese people value etiquette, and so do Westerners. For example, the Chinese "I want to book a meal" should not be rendered as "I want to…" here, but must use the polite question form "Can I / May I…". Likewise, when the server says very politely "How may I help you?", the customer should also be polite in reply, and add other responses such as "Perfect", "No problem", "Have a good day", etc. These are very necessary communicative expressions; you must be very familiar with them and their replies so you can say them fluently.

When the server asks how many people, the guest should not answer directly with a bare number such as "Two", but should say "A party of …". Here "party" does not mean "gathering" or "political party", but "companion, a group of people" — so "A party of …" actually means "a total of … people". In fact, when booking a flight ticket for yourself and a friend, the confirmation will show "xxx Party", where xxx is the surname. For example, if Mr. Zhou books a flight, the confirmation will show "Zhou Party", indicating the booking is for Mr. Zhou and his companions.

When making a reservation, the server will ask the guest's arrival time — remember to say morning or afternoon. If the requested time is answered with "not available", the guest may propose a preferred second time, e.g. "How about 8:00 p.m.?" If the server still finds no vacancy after checking, the guest may ask: "Would you please tell me what time is available?" and then choose a suitable time from those offered: "xxx a.m./p.m. is okay."

1. Instead of "I want to…", what polite form should a guest use to state their purpose when reserving abroad?
A polite question form such as "Can I…" or "May I…".
2. What polite responses should customers add to the conversation?
Responses such as "Perfect", "No problem", "Have a good day".
3. Instead of answering "Two" directly, what phrase should a guest use to state party size?
"A party of …", meaning a group of … people.
4. In an airline booking confirmation, what does "Zhou Party" indicate?
It indicates the booking is for Mr. Zhou and his travel companions.
5. If the requested time is "not available", what should the guest do?
Propose a second preferred time (e.g. "How about 8:00 p.m.?"); if still unavailable, ask "Would you please tell me what time is available?" then choose a suitable time.

任务3 问候及领位服务

Task 3: Greeting and Hostess Service
案例导入Case Introduction
"Hello, do you have a reservation?" The guest replied, "I am a staying guest; I already called to make a reservation just now." The hostess did not understand the guest's answer and kept asking whether he had a reservation. Somewhat impatiently, the guest told the hostess that when he booked by phone, the front desk told him he could come directly to this restaurant, and he showed his room card. After seeing the room card, the hostess immediately took the guest into the restaurant. At that moment, the restaurant manager came over; after asking, they learned that the guest had reserved a window seat in the hotel's Western restaurant… If you were the restaurant hostess, what would you do?
"您好，请问您有预订吗？"客人回答道："我是住店客人，刚才已经打电话预订过了。"领位员没有听明白客人的回答，继续询问客人是否有预订，客人有点不耐烦地告诉领位员，刚才电话预订时，前台告诉他可以直接到这个餐厅来用餐，并出示房卡。领位员看到客人房卡后，立刻将客人带进了餐厅。此时，餐厅经理走过来，经过询问才知道，客人预订的是酒店西餐厅靠窗的位置……如果你是餐厅的领位员，你会如何做呢？
知识准备Knowledge Preparation

Welcome Greetings

Before the restaurant opens, the service staff learn the reservation guest information of the day, especially VIP guest information, at the pre-meal meeting. For the restaurant hostess, checking and becoming familiar with the guests' reservation information before opening is the key work.

When the restaurant opens, the hostess stands at the door waiting for guests. When guests walk toward the door, the hostess should greet them with a smile and extend a sincere welcome. If meeting a frequent guest, the hostess can directly address the guest's last name and title, and converse appropriately, but should mind the propriety of language.

For unfamiliar guests, the hostess needs to ask if they have a reservation. When there is a reservation record in the system, guide the guest to the reserved table. If the guests do not have a reservation, it is necessary to ask how many guests there are, then arrange seats according to the number and lead them to the table.

The Process and Standard of Welcome Service (R24)

  • Pay attention to guests entering the restaurant; keep an eye on them at the entrance.
  • Within one minute of a guest entering, someone must step forward to greet him.
  • At a distance of 3 meters, greet the guest with a smile and eye contact.
  • Greet the guest, make a 15-degree bow and say: "Good morning / afternoon / evening, Mr./Ms. x x! Is there anything I can do for you?"
  • Ask the guest if they have a reservation, e.g. "Mr./Ms. x x, which private room did you book?" The tone must be soft and friendly, maintaining eye contact.
  • If no reservation, say "Let me check here if any table is available", then wait for the reply.
  • If no seats, express apology and offer other suggestions, e.g. "I'm so sorry, Mr./Ms. x x, the restaurant is full now. Would you mind waiting… to have dinner in our Chinese restaurant?"
  • If the guest accepts the suggestion, smile and say: "Just a moment, please. I'll check if there is a table available…"
  • If the whole restaurant confirms a seat, thank the guest for their patience and guide them there.
  • Explain the situation to the guests and keep them informed of the latest progress.

Hostess — Guide Guests to Their Seats

For guests without a reservation, if the restaurant attendance is not high, the hostess should carefully observe the number, identity, age, gender and preferences of guests to choose an appropriate table, and also consider the balance of restaurant space.

When guiding the guest, the hostess should match the guest's walking speed, lead in front of the guest with a moderate pace, keep an eye on the guest to make sure he/she is not far behind, remind the guest when meeting steps or turns, and use gestures to emphasize safety. After leading the guest to the table, first ask if the guest is satisfied with the arrangement. For frequent or VIP guests, try to place them in their customary area and table.

When the hostess guides guests to the table, the area server should take the initiative to greet them. The hostess should introduce the area server to the guests in time and assist them to sit. The specific operation: gently pull the chair with both hands and ask the guest to sit; when the guest is about to sit, gently push the chair forward. Priority goes to the principal guest; if this cannot be determined, serve the lady and the elderly first.

The Process and Standard of Guiding Service (R25)

  • Walk to the guest and greet in time; greet the guest first within 10 seconds; do not stay behind the front desk.
  • Say to the guest: "Good afternoon, Mr./Ms. x x, welcome." If the hostess knows the guest's name, speak it out loudly and clearly.
  • Ask for the guest's needs, e.g. "Is there anything I can do for you?"
  • If the guest wants to dine in the restaurant, ask if they already have a reservation; a guest with a reservation should be guided to the table within 1 minute.
  • Ask the number of diners and preferred table, then check the system to ensure the table is not double-booked; a guest without a reservation should be guided to the table within 2 minutes.
  • Make sure the guest is following and say "Mr./Ms. x x, this way please." Walk in front of the guest, not too fast, keep the guest on the right side, and confirm the guest is following.
  • Stop at the table, smile and ask if the guest is satisfied with the table, then pull out the chair for the guest.
  • Move slowly and carefully. When the guest is ready to sit, put both hands on the sides of the chair and push it in with your right hand.
  • Wish the guest enjoy the food.

迎宾问候

餐厅服务员在开餐前例会时，会了解到当日预订的客人信息，特别是VIP客人信息。对于餐厅领位员来讲，在开餐前查阅并熟悉预订客人的相关预订信息是工作的重点。

餐厅开始营业后，领位员站在餐厅门口等候宾客的到来。当客人走向餐厅门口时，领位员要面带微笑，主动与客人打招呼，对客人的到来致以诚挚的欢迎。若客人为常客，则可直接称呼客人的姓氏加职位头衔，并可适当与客人寒暄，但要注意语言分寸。

对于不熟悉的客人，领位员要询问客人是否有预订。如果客人有预订，查询预订系统相关记录，再将客人引导至所预订的桌位。如果客人没有预订，则需要询问客人有几位，根据客人人数来安排座位，并将客人引导至餐桌。

迎宾服务的流程及标准（R24）

  • 注意步入餐厅的客人，在入口处留意客人。
  • 客人进入餐厅1分钟内，须有人上前迎接问候。
  • 距离3米时，面带微笑、目光接触地向客人迎上问候。
  • 问候客人，行15度鞠躬礼并说："早上/下午/晚上好，x x先生/女士！请问有什么可以帮您？"
  • 询问客人是否预订，如"x x先生/女士，请问您预订的是哪个包间？"语气须轻妥、友好，建立目光联系。
  • 如无预订，说"我看一下是否还有空余的桌位"，然后等待答复。
  • 如无空位，向客人表示歉意并提出其他建议，如"十分抱歉，x x先生/女士，餐厅现在已经客满。您介意……去我们的中餐厅用餐吗？"
  • 若客人接受提议，微笑说："请稍等，我查一下是否有空位……"
  • 若整个餐厅确认有位，感谢客人的耐心等候并引导前往。
  • 向客人解释情况，并让他们不断了解最新进度。

领位（引客入座）

对于没有预订的客人，若餐厅上座率不高，领位员要仔细观察客人的人数、身份、年龄、性别、喜好来选择适当的餐位，同时还要考虑餐厅空间的平衡。

引导客人时，领位员要配合客人走路的速度，以适度的步伐在客人侧前方带领，不时以目光关注客人是否紧随其后。遇到台阶或转弯时，应提醒客人并以手势加强，请客人注意安全。引导客人至桌位后，应先询问客人对餐位的安排是否满意。对于常客或VIP客人，要尽量将其安排到其习惯的区域与桌位。

领位员引客入座时，区域服务员要主动上前问候，领位员要及时将区域服务员介绍给客人认识，并协助客人入座。具体操作是：用双手帮客人轻轻拉开椅子，请客人入座，当客人将要坐下时，将椅子轻轻往前推送。拉椅顺序以主宾优先，若无法判断，则以女士及年长者优先。

领位服务的流程及标准（R25）

  • 走向客人，及时致意；须在10秒钟内向客人致意，不要停留在前台后。
  • 主动对客人说："下午好，x x先生/女士，欢迎您……"若领位员知道客人姓名，应大声清晰地说出。
  • 询问客人的要求，如"有什么需要帮忙的吗？"
  • 若客人想在餐厅用餐，询问是否已预订；有预订的客人须在1分钟内被引领至餐桌。
  • 询问就餐人数及偏好餐位，再查系统确认未重订；无预订客人须在2分钟内被引领至餐桌。
  • 请客人跟随自己，说："x x先生/女士，请随我来。"在客人前面走，不要过快，让客人位于右侧，不时确认客人跟随。
  • 在桌边停下，微笑询问客人对餐位是否满意，然后为客人拉椅。
  • 动作要慢、要小心。当客人准备落座时，双手扶椅侧，用右手将椅子推入。
  • 祝愿客人用餐愉快。
任务实施Task Implementation

Step 1: The teacher introduces the scenario of this section.

Scenario 1: Ms. Lucy and her party of 4 people arrive at the restaurant at 8:30 p.m. Jen, the restaurant hostess, greets the guests at the door. After asking the guests' information, she leads them to the Prince's Room and introduces the service staff to the guests.

Scenario 2: Mr. Wang and his party of 4 people come to the restaurant without a reservation. What should the hostess do when the restaurant has vacancy? What should she do when the restaurant has no vacancy and the guests need to wait?

Step 2: The teacher lists relevant phrases or sentence patterns for students' reference:

  • — Do you have a reservation, sir / ma'am?（先生/女士，请问有预订吗？）
  • — Let me see if there is any table available.（让我看一下是否还有空余的桌位。）
  • — May I have your name, please?（请问怎么称呼您？）
  • — I'm glad we have a table available.
  • — I'm very sorry, the restaurant is full now, Mr./Ms. x x. There will be a table available in x x minutes. Would you mind waiting for a moment?（十分抱歉，x x先生/女士，餐厅现在已经满了。x x分钟以后会有空位，您介意等候片刻吗？）
  • — We're fully booked tonight. Would you like to go to our Chinese restaurant?（这儿今晚已经订满了。您乐意去我们的中餐厅用餐吗？）
  • — Just a moment please. I'll show you if there's a vacant seat over there.
  • — Please follow me.
  • — Hope you enjoy the meal.

Step 3: The teacher gives a demonstration case and sets an example.

Step 4: Take the group (5 people per group) as a unit, in which one student acts as the hostess and the other four act as guests, respectively simulating greeting the guests and guiding them to their seats (5 students take turns to do scene simulation).
