拓展阅读Extended Reading

How to Solve the Restaurant Queuing and Waiting Problem

Researchers chose two restaurants near Beijing's Workers' Stadium to study the queuing phenomenon and concluded: chained restaurants of a certain scale all manage queuing, with a customer loss rate of about 20%; smaller but equally distinctive restaurants that do not manage queuing have a loss rate of over 50%.

1. Causes of queuing. The production and consumption of service occur simultaneously, so demand fluctuation is inevitable. Guests arrive randomly and expect immediate service. If all service resources are occupied on arrival, the guest must wait patiently. Natural demand fluctuations, especially simultaneous peaks, leave the restaurant idle at some times and force waiting at others — forming concentrated peak periods such as 11:30–12:30 and 18:30–19:40. Service capacity is limited: unlike goods stored in a warehouse, a restaurant's capacity (seats, table design, process) is fixed at initial investment. Homogeneity or difference between restaurants also affects choice through price sensitivity, taste, environment and other psychological factors.

2. Psychology of waiting guests. The cost of waiting is the alternative activities forgone, plus boredom, anxiety and other reactions. Guests first feel anxiety (am I valued? when is my turn? will I be treated fairly?). Then they feel bored and idle, which is unpleasant. Unfair treatment causes dissatisfaction and may turn a guest into a critical, difficult consumer.

3. Principles of queue management. Guests are a potential resource in the service process; losing even a few daily accumulates into declining revenue. Therefore, setting a queuing strategy, shortening perceived waiting time and eliminating negative effects are basic duties of the operator. (1) Let guests know staff know they are waiting — assign an employee to contact waiting guests. (2) Make waiting pleasant — set a dedicated, quiet and elegant waiting area with music. (3) Use waiting time effectively — guests observe surroundings closely, so mind details. (4) Establish clear queuing rules — guests receive a sequential number and wait to be called.

4. Methods of queue management. (1) Set a dedicated waiting area with comfortable sofas and small tables; a attached bar can increase revenue. (2) Decorate the waiting area separately, quiet and elegant, with bright colors and relaxing music; display new products, provide newspapers, and post promotional and staff-achievement boards. (3) Shift demand by differential pricing — discount guests who finish within a fixed time, speeding turnover before/at the start of the queue (e.g. settling between 11:30–11:40) to free seats. (4) Manage the flow so waiting guests rarely leave and dining guests are not disturbed. (5) Analyze the dining process: waiting time runs from queuing for a seat until settlement; use waiting time well to find continuous profit points.

1. According to the survey near Beijing's Workers' Stadium, what was the difference in customer loss rates between managed chain restaurants and unmanaged small restaurants?
Managed chain restaurants had about a 20% loss rate; unmanaged small distinctive restaurants had over 50%.
2. What are the main causes of queuing described in the passage?
Simultaneous demand fluctuation creating peak periods, limited service capacity, and store homogeneity/difference affecting choice.
3. What psychological reactions do waiting guests experience?
Anxiety (about being valued, turn, fairness) and boredom/idleness; unfair treatment causes dissatisfaction.
4. What principles of queue management are listed?
Let guests know staff know they are waiting; make waiting pleasant; use waiting time effectively; establish clear queuing rules (numbered tickets).
5. Name two methods suggested for managing the waiting area.
Set a dedicated waiting area with comfortable sofas/tables (and an attached bar); decorate it to be quiet and elegant with bright colors, relaxing music, newspapers and promotional boards. Also use differential pricing to shift demand.

项目三 餐前准备

Project 3 Preparation
项目概述Project Overview
In a restaurant, staff members in different positions all have their own duties, which are trivial and specific. These tasks are collectively called pre-meal preparation.
在餐厅中，不同岗位的工作人员都有其分内的工作，这些工作都是烦琐又具体的。这些工作被统称为餐前准备工作。
Before the restaurant opens, the restaurant manager or supervisor will hold a pre-meal meeting, where service staff receive task assignments, learn about the areas they are to serve, the day's reservations, the needs of regular and VIP guests and service precautions, and become familiar with the menu and the day's specials and recommended dishes. After the pre-meal meeting, staff in each position inspect the hygiene of their assigned areas and the preparation of items at the service station.
在餐厅营业之前，餐厅经理或者主管会召开餐前例会，服务人员接受任务分配，了解自己所要服务的区域、餐厅当日的预订情况，掌握当日常客及 VIP 客人的需求及服务注意事项，熟悉菜单及当日特选菜、推荐菜等。餐前例会结束后，各岗位工作人员检查当值区域卫生及服务工作台物品准备情况。
Adequate pre-meal preparation is the beginning of good service. Even though each restaurant differs in size and nature, in order to operate effectively, staff must strictly follow various regulations to ensure standardized arrangement and procedural operations.
充分的餐前准备工作是良好服务的开端。即使每家餐厅规模不同、性质各异，但为了有效经营，工作人员必须严格执行各种规程，做到布置规范化、操作程序化。
All pre-meal preparations before the restaurant opens are related to the day's operation. If pre-meal preparation is not done well, causing guests to wait after arrival, it will make guests feel that the restaurant's service is unprofessional and inattentive.
餐厅营业前的各项餐前准备工作关系着餐厅当日的运作。若是餐前准备工作没有做好，致使客人抵达后需要等待，将会让客人感觉餐厅的服务不专业、不用心。
项目目标Project Objectives
项目目标 Project Objectives
类别Category内容Content
知识目标Knowledge Objectives了解餐前准备的工作项目，了解餐前例会的内容；掌握托盘、餐巾折花的基本知识与方法。Understand the items of pre-meal preparation and the contents of pre-meal meetings; master the basic knowledge and methods of tray service and napkin folding.
技能目标Skill Objectives掌握托盘的使用方法；掌握常见中、西餐餐巾折花技能。Master the use of trays; master common Chinese and Western napkin folding skills.
素质目标Quality Objectives能够准确地列举出餐前准备、服务准备阶段的工作重点，提高自己的专业能力，能够根据不同情况选择花型并摆放得当。Be able to accurately list the key points of pre-meal preparation and service preparation, improve professional ability, and be able to choose flower shapes and place them appropriately according to different situations.
思政目标Ideological Objectives具备精益求精的工匠精神；具备较强的服务意识、安全意识；具有较强的责任心与严谨的工作作风。Possess the craftsmanship spirit of striving for perfection; have strong service awareness and safety awareness; have a strong sense of responsibility and rigorous work style.

任务1 掌握餐前准备工作

Task 1: Master Pre-meal Preparation
案例导入Case Introduction
Mr. Wang, a VIP guest of a hotel, called the hotel's Chinese restaurant. At noon the next day at 12:00, he would host a very important French guest at the Chinese restaurant. The Chinese restaurant attached great importance to Mr. Wang's call. At the pre-meal meeting, the restaurant manager once again emphasized the precautions for receiving VIP guests at noon, and specially assigned Joe, who is experienced and attentive, to be responsible for this VIP reception. After the meeting, Joe began to check the item preparation and hygiene of his work area. He inadvertently saw the table setting on the table and immediately called the manager, who immediately agreed to his rectification plan.
某酒店的 VIP 客人王先生致电酒店中餐厅，次日中午 12:00 他将在中餐厅宴请一位十分重要的法国客人。中餐厅十分重视王先生的来电，在餐前例会时，餐厅经理再次强调了中午接待 VIP 客人的注意事项，并且特意分配了工作经验丰富、服务细致周到的乔负责此次 VIP 接待。例会结束后，乔开始检查工作区域的物品准备及卫生情况。他不经意看到了餐桌上的摆台，立刻给经理打了一个电话，经理马上同意了他的整改方案。
It turned out that Joe saw that the table was still set with traditional Chinese restaurant tableware. Thinking that the guest was French and might not know how to use chopsticks, helping the guest change utensils after the meal started would reduce the restaurant's service quality. Therefore, he added a knife and fork at the main guest's place.
原来，乔看到餐桌上还是摆着中餐厅的传统餐具，想到客人是一位法国人，有可能不会用筷子，等开餐后再去帮客人更换餐具，会降低餐厅的服务品质。于是，他在主宾的手位补充上了刀叉。
At noon, Mr. Wang walked into the restaurant with his French friend. When he saw the tableware different from usual, Mr. Wang praised the hotel's thoughtfulness. His French friend also praised: "I didn't tell the restaurant in advance that I can't use chopsticks. You are really amazing!"
中午，王先生带着他的法国朋友走进了餐厅，当看到餐位上不同往常的餐具时，王先生连连赞扬酒店的用心。而他的法国朋友也称赞地说：“我没有提前告知餐厅我不会用筷子，你们真是神机妙算！”
Thinking and discussion: If Joe had not thought of preparing a knife and fork for the guest, and had prepared it only after the guest was seated according to his needs, what would be the result?
思考讨论：如果乔没有想到为客人准备刀叉，在客人入座后根据其需求再准备，结果会怎样？
知识准备Knowledge Preparation

1. Pre-meal Meetings

In order to provide employees with timely information and better service to guests, the restaurant should require employees to attend regular pre-meal meetings on time before each shift begins. The regular meeting is usually presided over by the restaurant manager or supervisor. The main contents of the regular pre-meal meeting are as follows:

一、餐前例会

为了员工可以及时获取信息、更好地为客人提供服务，在每个班次开始工作前，餐厅应要求员工准时参加例会。例会一般由餐厅经理或主管主持。餐前例会的主要内容如下：
表 3.1 餐前例会的流程和标准 The Process and Standard of Pre-meal Meetings
步骤Step流程Process标准Standard
11 开例会并告知员工相关的信息Hold regular meetings to inform related information 餐厅的活动和相关安排；有关同事的正面信息（如奖励等）；VIP 客人的名单，分享客人特殊爱好；当日预订；每日特选、应季食品；餐饮推广活动（新的计划和取得的进展）；沽清项目（食品、酒水）；上座率（人数、收入、平均消费）；行动要点（重要的、需要马上解决的事情）；仪容仪表检查；员工工作任务分派。 Activities and related arrangement; Good information about colleagues (e.g. rewards); List of VIP guests and their special preference; Reservation; Daily specials and seasonal food; Catering promotion activities (new plans and progress); Sold-out item (food and beverage); Attendance rate (number of people, income and average consumption); Action points (important and urgent things); Appearance check; Job assignment.
22 反馈信息和跟进Feedback and follow up 搜集员工的反馈意见并记录，如有可能及时跟进。 Collect feedback from employees, write down the suggestions and follow up if possible.
The pre-meal regular meeting should not take too long, generally about 10 minutes. There must be a fixed meeting time every day. Usually, the lunch pre-meal meeting is held at 10:00 a.m. or 10:30 a.m., and the dinner pre-meal meeting is held at 4:00 p.m. or 4:30 p.m. The manager or supervisor should be well prepared before the meeting and write down the main points to be discussed in advance. During the meeting, all staff should line up and be encouraged with an attitude that expects them to do a good job. The manager or supervisor should speak clearly and create a relaxed atmosphere so that employees can easily accept it. In addition, the manager or supervisor should regularly invite leaders to guide the meeting, convey superior instructions in a timely manner, and ensure that upper-level directives are passed down and lower-level feedback is reported up.
餐前例会时间不应太长，一般以 10 分钟左右为宜。每天要有固定的开会时间，通常午餐餐前例会在上午 10:00 或 10:30 进行，晚餐餐前例会在下午 4:00 或 4:30 进行。经理或主管开例会前要做好充分的准备，事先写下开会时要讲的工作要点。开会时要求员工列队，要以期望员工做好服务工作的态度去激励员工，讲话要清晰，气氛要轻松，让员工易于接受。另外，要定期请上级领导到会指导，及时传达上级的指示，做到上情下达、下情上报。

2. Preparations

Pre-meal preparation is a series of preparations completed by restaurant servers before guests' arrival according to service procedures. It is the beginning of good service. The following preparations should be done:

二、准备工作

餐前准备工作是餐厅服务人员在客人到达之前，按服务程序完成的一系列服务准备工作，是做好服务工作的开始。具体应做好如下准备：
表 3.2 准备工作的内容和标准 The Content and Standard of Preparations
准备内容Content工作标准Standard
环境及清洁卫生Environment and cleaning 餐厅大门及周围环境干净整齐，地面、餐桌椅等干净无尘；餐具柜、服务车等符合卫生要求；餐具干净卫生，无破损；餐厅室温适宜，根据季节及营业时间将温度控制在 18℃～22℃之间。 The restaurant door and the surrounding environment are clean and tidy. The floor, dining tables and chairs are clean and dust-free; Sideboards and service carts should meet the hygiene requirements; Tableware is clean and without damage; Dining room temperature is appropriate. According to the season and operating time, control the temperature between 18℃-22℃.
设备检查Equipment inspection 开餐前 1 小时检查所有照明设备、空调、背景音乐开关及音响设备是否正常；调节好室内灯光及音响。 Check whether all lighting equipment, air conditioner, background music switch and sound equipment are normal running one hour before the meal; Adjust indoor lighting and sound.
餐具、服务用品准备Tableware and service supplies preparation 将所需要的餐用具消毒后放在备餐间或备餐桌上；准备好服务用品（如各种托盘、开瓶工具、餐巾、牙签等）。 Put the necessary tableware on the preparation room or table after disinfection; Prepare service items, such as trays, bottle openers, napkins, toothpicks, etc.
摆台Table setting 符合餐厅摆放要求；餐具干净卫生、无破损。 Comply with the restaurant display requirements; Tableware is clean and without damage.
酒水饮料准备Beverage preparation 备好供应的酒水饮料、茶叶、开水、冰块等；检查酒水饮料的质量，发现问题，及时退回。 Prepare beverage, tea, boiled water, ice, etc.; Check the quality of beverage. If there is any problem, return it in time.
菜单准备Menu preparation 备好零点及套餐菜单；熟悉菜单的内容；熟悉当天菜单的变化。 Prepare a la carte and set menu; Be familiar with the menu; Be familiar with daily menu changes.
员工形象准备Employees' image preparation 餐厅员工仪容仪表符合餐厅工作服务要求。 The appearance of the restaurant staff should match the restaurant service requirement.
检查预订摆台Check the reservation table 所摆餐位符合预订人数，摆放符合要求；指示牌、可用菜单干净，内容正确无误；餐台鲜花新鲜、美观。 The table is arranged according to the number of reservation and the table setting requirements; The signpost and menu are clean and correct; The flowers on the table are fresh and beautiful.
准备迎接宾客Get ready for the guests 一般由领位或迎宾员在开餐前 5 分钟站在餐厅门后，准备迎接宾客。 Generally, the hostess stands behind the door of the restaurant 5 minutes before the meal and prepares to greet the guests.
In addition, due to the age, occupation, identity, nationality, region, gender and other factors, the purposes, standards and requirements of the restaurant guests are different. Restaurant servers should be all eyes and all ears, and pay attention to everywhere, be careful at all times, be careful in everything, be good at observing and estimating the eyes, expressions, manners and actions of the guests, and be psychologically prepared for all kinds of guests' requests during meals. The service should vary from person to person. The servers should grasp a good scale to make sure the service is to the point.
此外，来餐厅用餐的客人，由于年龄、职业、身份、国籍、地区、性别等不同，用餐目的、标准及要求也各不相同。餐厅服务员要眼观六路、耳听八方，做到处处留心、时时细心、事事精心，要善于观察和判断客人的眼神、表情、举止、动作，要对各类宾客的各种用餐要求有心理准备，要因人而异，掌握好尺度，使服务接待工作恰到好处。
任务实施Task Implementation

Step 1: According to the teacher's explanation, the groups set up their own pre-meal meeting situation.

Step 2: The teacher prompts the key points of simulation training, including the content setting, the regular meeting process, the regular meeting main points and so on.

Step 3: Take the group (8 people per group) as a unit, in which one student acts as the restaurant manager, the other seven students play the role of restaurant server, respectively simulates the regular pre-meal meeting (eight students take turns to carry on the scene simulation).

第一步：根据教师的知识讲解，各小组自行设定餐前例会情景。

第二步：教师提示模拟训练要点，包括内容的设定、例会的流程、例会的要点等。

第三步：以小组（8 人 1 组）为单位，其中，1 位学生扮演餐厅经理，另外 7 位学生扮演餐厅服务人员，分别模拟召开餐前例会（8 位学生轮流进行情景模拟）。

拓展阅读Extended Reading

Psychological Analysis of Guests' Basic Needs

For most hotel guests, dining in a hotel is both a need and an enjoyment. The psychological analysis of guests' basic needs for food and beverage is as follows.

1. Nutrition

Nutrition can improve people's normal physiological functions and disease resistance. Whether nutrition is high or low and whether the collocation is reasonable directly affect people's energy state and work efficiency, and even affect their appearance and personality. Since nutrition is inseparable from the quality of daily meals, guests hope that the dishes provided by the restaurant can meet their scientific nutritional needs, and require that the nutritional components and content of the meals be indicated. Nowadays guests also hope to eat more green food.

2. Flavor

Flavor refers to the overall impression that guests have of dishes or other foods when dining, and it is the most important factor affecting food selection. Flavor is the comprehensive feeling of taste, smell and texture tasted by the eater.

3. Hygiene

Guests pay great attention to the hygiene of tableware and the dining environment. Hygiene is the basic physiological need of guests. Restaurants should attach importance to hygiene to ensure that guests are not threatened by diseases. It should be emphasized that every practitioner in the catering industry must strictly abide by the hygiene regulations formulated by the catering department. In the eyes of guests, the cleanliness of service personnel is also an important part of restaurant hygiene.

Therefore, in order to meet the needs of dining guests, service personnel should not only make guests feel "at home", but also master business knowledge proficiently and cater to guests' needs for nutrition and flavor. At the same time, for guests' needs for speed and hygiene, they should try to shorten guests' waiting time and do a good job of cleaning and sanitation.

1. What are the three basic needs of restaurant guests discussed in the passage?
Nutrition, flavor and hygiene.
2. Why do guests care about nutrition?
Because nutrition affects physiological function, disease resistance, energy, work efficiency, appearance and personality.
3. What does "flavor" mean in the passage?
It is the overall impression of taste, smell and texture that a guest experiences when eating.
4. Why is hygiene important in a restaurant?
It is a basic physiological need; it protects guests from disease and includes both the environment/tableware and the cleanliness of staff.
5. What should servers do to meet guests' needs?
Make guests feel at home, master professional knowledge, meet nutrition and flavor needs, shorten waiting time, and maintain cleanliness.

任务2 托盘服务

Task 2: Tray Service
案例导入Case Introduction
Xiao Wang is a junior student at a tourism college. It was his first time working in a hotel as a restaurant server. Having never worked independently, Xiao Wang was in a hurry and put all the drinks and beverages the guests needed on the tray. As a result, when passing drinks to the guests, he accidentally knocked over the drinks placed on the outside, causing paper cups and drinks to spill all over the floor and wetting a guest's clothes.
小王是某旅游院校的一名大三学生。初次进入酒店做餐厅服务员，从来没有独立工作过的小王手忙脚乱，把客人需要的酒水、饮料一股脑儿地全放在了托盘上，结果在给客人传递酒水时，不小心将放在外侧的饮料打翻，致使纸杯和酒水洒了一地，并且把一位客人的衣服给弄湿了。
Thinking and discussion: If you were Xiao Wang, what should you do? If you were Xiao Wang's supervisor, how should you train Xiao Wang's tray service skills in the future?
思考讨论：如果你是小王，你应该怎么做？如果你是小王的领班，今后应该如何培训小王的端托服务技能？
知识准备Knowledge Preparation
The tray is one of the common tools used to transport all kinds of items in restaurant service. Therefore, the proficiency of tray usage is very important. Mastering tray operation skills can improve work efficiency, ensure service quality and standardize the service of the restaurant.
托盘是餐厅服务工作中用于运送各种物品的常用工具之一。因此，托盘操作的熟练程度十分重要。熟练掌握托盘操作技能可以提高工作效率、保证服务质量和规范餐厅的服务工作。

1. Types and Usage of Trays

(1) Types of trays

① According to the manufacture material of the tray, it can be divided into wood tray, metal tray and bakelite anti-slip tray.

② According to the size and shape, it can be divided into large, medium and small three sizes of rectangular and round tray. The three sizes of round tray are as follows: the large tray has a diameter of more than 36cm; the diameter of the middle tray is 32cm-36cm; the tray with a diameter of 20cm-32cm is the small tray. Rectangular trays are also divided into three sizes according to the specification: large, medium and small.

一、托盘的种类与用途

（一）托盘的种类

① 按照托盘的制作材料，可分为木托盘、金属托盘和胶木防滑托盘。

② 按照托盘的规格和形状，可分为大、中、小三种规格的长方形托盘和圆托盘。圆托盘中，直径大于 36cm 的为大圆托盘，直径为 32cm～36cm 的为中圆托盘；直径为 20cm～32cm 的为小圆托盘。长方托盘也按此规格分大、中、小三种。

(2) Usage of trays

① The large and medium rectangular trays are used to deliver heavy dishes, wine and tableware.

② Small rectangular tray, large and medium round tray, are usually used for table setting, pouring wine, serving dishes and drinks, etc.

③ Small round tray and 6 inch (15.24cm) silver dish are mainly used to send bills, money, letters and other light items.

（二）托盘的用途

① 大、中长方托盘，一般用于托运分量较重的菜点、酒水，收运餐具等重的器皿。

② 小方盘和大、中圆盘，一般用于摆台、斟酒、上菜、上饮料等。

③ 小圆盘和 6 寸（15.24cm）银盘，主要用于递送账单、钱币、信札及小礼品等轻便物品。

2. The Use of Trays

According to the weight of the items, there are two ways: light holder and heavy holder. If the weight of items is within 5kg, it is suitable to use light holder; if the weight of the goods is above 5kg, it is suitable to use heavy holder.

二、托盘的使用

按所托物品轻重，有轻托和重托两种方式。物品重量在 5kg 以内的，适宜采用轻托方式；物品重量在 5kg 以上的，则采用重托方式。
(1) Light holder
Light holder is also called "chest holder". This method is more used with medium and small trays, which is easy to work. The main points of the light holder are as follows:

① Keep your shoulders parallel, and use your left hand.

② The angle between the upper arm and the lower arm is 90°.

③ The palm faces upward, the fingers are open, the fingertips and palm base support the bottom of the tray, and the palm is hollow.

④ The elbow is 15cm from the waist.

⑤ The right hand naturally droops or is placed behind the back.

（一）轻托
轻托又称“胸前托”。此法多用中、小型托盘，易于工作。轻托的动作要领如下：

① 肩平行，用左手。

② 上臂与前臂成 90°。

③ 手掌向上，五指分开，用五指指尖和掌根托住托盘底部，掌心虚空。

④ 手肘离腰部 15cm。

⑤ 右手自然下垂或放于背后。

(2) Heavy holder
Heavy holder is also called "shoulder holder". This method is usually used with large trays. The key points of the heavy holder are as follows:

① Use the left hand.

② While bending the left elbow upward, turn the palm 180° to the left to above the shoulder. The palm stands about 2cm above the shoulder; the fingers are naturally separated, and the balance of the tray is controlled with the fingers and palm base.

③ The position of the tray should be such that the bottom does not press the shoulder, the edge is not close to the mouth, and the back does not touch the hair.

④ The right hand should swing naturally or hold the front inner corner of the tray.

At present, in order to serve with safety and convenience, catering enterprises generally do not use large trays; instead, they use small carts to deliver heavy items.

（二）重托
重托又称“肩上托”。此法多用于大型托盘。重托的动作要领如下：

① 用左手。

② 左手向上弯曲臂肘的同时，手掌向左转动手腕 180° 至肩上方。手掌略高出肩 2cm，五指自然分开，用五指和掌根部控制托盘的平衡。

③ 托盘的位置以盘底不压肩、盘缘不近嘴、盘后不靠发为准。

④ 右手应自然下垂摆动或扶住托盘的前内角。

目前，为了安全省力，餐饮企业一般不采用大型托盘，多用小型手推车递送重物。

表 3.4 托盘的使用步骤、技能要求和操作规范 The Process, Skill Requirement and Operation Specification of the Use of Trays
步骤Process技能要求Skill Requirement操作规范Operation Specification
理盘Preparing 1. 根据装运物品选择托盘；2. 垫上垫布；3. 防滑。1. Pick the tray based on the items; 2. Pad the placemat; 3. Non-slip. 1. 托盘应洗净、擦干；2. 垫布平铺整齐。1. The trays should be clean and dry; 2. The placemat should be tiled and neat.
装盘Setting 根据物品形状、体积、使用先后顺序等进行合理装盘。According to the shape, volume, usage order of the object to set the tray. 1. 较重、较高的物品放在里面，较轻、较低物品放在外面；2. 先用的物品放在前面、上面，后用的物品放在里面、下面；3. 重心分布合理。1. Put heavier and taller items inside and the lighter and lower objects outside; 2. Put the prior used items in the front and above; put others inside and below; 3. The weight should be well distributed.
起盘Taking 保持托盘的平稳，汤汁不洒、菜肴不变形。Keep the tray stable, the soup not spill out, dishes non-deformable. 1. 将托盘的 1/3 托至操作台外；2. 左手托住托盘底部，掌心位于底部中间，右手握住托盘；3. 如托盘较重，则先屈膝，用力托住托盘后起身，然后用手掌托住盘底。1. Place one third of the tray outside the counter; 2. The left hand holds the bottom of the tray, puts the palm in the middle of the bottom, and the right hand holds the tray; 3. If the tray is heavy, bend the knee first, then lift the tray up, and use the palm to hold the bottom.
托盘行走Walking 步伐轻盈、稳健，汤汁不洒，身体挺直、略向前倾。The pace is light and steady, the soup can not be spilled out. The body should be straight and slightly forward. 行走时要注意周围情况，能较好控制行走速度。Pay attention to the surroundings, control the walking speed.

拓展阅读Extended Reading

The Importance of Trays

Restaurant operators put a lot of effort into shaping the restaurant environment, but often overlook a detail: the tray, an important tool that must be used in restaurant service. Exquisite dishes matched with suitable trays can better highlight the restaurant's taste.

Suiyuan Shidan records that "delicious food is not as good as beautiful utensils", and points out that after dishes are cooked, "stir-fried dishes should be served on plates, soups should be served in bowls, and arranged alternately to make them look vivid". This is a concise summary of fine food, fine utensils and fine decoration. Many restaurant operators think it is enough to provide high-end tableware for guests, but this is not the case. If the matching of trays is included in the consideration of beautiful utensils, the overall effect will be better.

1. Why do restaurant operators often overlook the tray?
They focus more on shaping the restaurant environment.
2. How can a suitable tray improve restaurant service?
Matching exquisite dishes with suitable trays can highlight the restaurant's taste.
3. What does "delicious food is not as good as beautiful utensils" mean?
The presentation and serving vessels are as important as the food itself.
4. According to the passage, what kinds of utensils are suitable for different dishes?
Stir-fried dishes should be served on plates and soups in bowls.
5. What is the author's suggestion for restaurant operators?
They should include tray matching in the consideration of beautiful utensils to achieve a better overall effect.

任务3 餐巾折花

Task 3: Napkin Folding
案例导入Case Introduction
Xiao Wang from the banquet department of a hotel received a Japanese guest, Mr. Koizumi. After learning about the hotel's service in detail, Mr. Koizumi booked a high-end banquet for 40 people three days later, put forward banquet requirements and paid a deposit. Three days later, the banquet department made all preparations according to Mr. Koizumi's requirements, and chose elegant lotus-shaped napkin folding according to the season. When the greeter led several Japanese guests to the dining table, the guests were very displeased.
某饭店宴会部的小王接待了一位日本客人小泉先生。小泉先生详细了解了酒店的服务情况后，预订了 3 天后 40 人的高档宴会，并提出了宴会要求，交付了订金。3 天后宴会部按照小泉先生的要求做好了一切准备工作，并根据时令季节选用了优雅的荷花造型的餐巾折花。当迎宾员将其中的几位日本客人引领到餐桌时，客人十分不悦。
Thinking and discussion: Why were the guests displeased? If you were a waiter in the banquet department, what should you do next?
思考讨论：客人为什么不悦？如果你是该宴会部的服务员，接下来该怎么做？
知识准备Knowledge Preparation

1. Napkins and Napkin Folding Flowers

A napkin, also called "mouth cloth", is a square cloth towel, and is placed under a plate or on the lap during the meal. Napkins are a kind of cleaning equipment that guests can use to wipe off oil stains on their hands or mouth to prevent alcohol or soup dripping on their clothes.

一、餐巾及餐巾折花

餐巾又称“口布”，是一种正方形布巾。客人在用餐过程中将餐巾压置于骨碟下面或者置于大腿上。餐巾是餐桌上常备的一种保洁用品，客人可以用其擦去手上或嘴上的油渍，防止酒水、汤汁滴洒到衣服上。
The earliest napkin folding flowers were directly folded into a square and put flat on the plate. Until 1639, the Italians developed napkin folding into an ornamental art. The most important role of napkins is cleaning, so from the view of hygiene, napkin flower patterns should be as simple as possible. At the same time, simple napkin folding can also save service time and is convenient for guests to open and use.
最早的餐巾折花是直接折成正方形，平放在餐盘上。直至 1639 年，意大利人将餐巾折叠发展成为一种观赏艺术。餐巾最重要的作用是清洁，因此，从卫生角度出发，餐巾折花花样越简单越好。同时，简单的餐巾折花也可以节省服务人员的时间，方便客人打开使用。
In order to create atmosphere and beautify the dining table, sometimes the restaurant needs to choose napkins of different colors and shapes to fold flowers. As a result, there will be various animal and plant shapes made by napkins on the table.
餐厅为了营造气氛、美化餐桌，有时候需要选择不同颜色、不同造型的餐巾折花，因此，餐桌上会出现各种不同动植物造型的餐巾折花。

2. Types of Napkin Folding Flowers

Napkin folding flowers can be divided into three types: cup flower, plate flower and ring flower. The cup flower is folded and placed in a water cup; it will unfold when taken out of the cup, and is often used in Chinese restaurant table settings and various Chinese banquets. The plate flower can be placed independently on the plate or on the dining table after folding, and is often used in Western restaurants. Because plate flowers are easy to fold and can be shaped independently, they are also common in Chinese restaurants. The ring flower is rolled flat or folded into shape and placed in a napkin ring. Napkin ring, also known as napkin buckle, can be made of plastic, porcelain, silver, bone, ivory, etc. In addition, napkin rings can also be replaced by colored ribbons or silk cords. Ring flowers are usually placed on decorative plates or dinner plates to give guests a traditional, concise and elegant feel, and can be used in both Chinese and Western restaurants.

二、餐巾折花的种类

餐巾折花可分为杯花、盘花与环花三种。杯花是将餐巾折好造型之后放置于水杯中，水杯去除之后即散开，常用于中餐厅摆台及各类中式宴席。盘花折叠成形后，可独立放置于餐盘中或者餐桌上，常用于西餐厅。由于盘花折叠简单、可独立成型，因此也常见于中餐厅。环花是将餐巾平整卷好或者折叠成造型后套在餐巾环内。餐巾环也称为餐巾扣，材料有塑料、瓷质、银制、骨制、象牙等。除此之外，餐巾环还可以用彩色丝带或丝绳带代替。餐巾环花通常放置在装饰盘或餐盘上，给人以传统、简洁和雅致的感觉，中西餐厅皆可使用。
Western restaurants prefer white and simple-style napkins. The napkins used in Chinese restaurants are more changeable and colourful. Pure cotton is the most popular napkin material. It is not only because it has good water absorption, but also the flower folding is easy to shape. Napkins of blended material have poor water absorption and are not easy to form when folding flowers.
西餐厅比较偏爱白色且样式简单的餐巾，中餐厅的餐巾变化较多，色彩也较为丰富。餐巾的材质以纯棉最受欢迎，这种材质不仅吸水性好，而且折花易于成型。混纺材质的餐巾吸水性差，且折花时不易成型。

3. The Display Requirements of Table Napkin Flowers

Napkin flowers are widely used from state banquets to family dinners. The overall furnishing requirements are neat and beautiful, properly matched, easy to use, easy to watch, and as much as possible combined with the tones of the tablecloth and tableware. The following points should be noted when placing napkin flowers:

三、餐巾花的摆放要求

餐巾花的应用比较广泛，大至国宴小到家宴都可以用餐巾花来装饰。总的摆设要求是整齐美观、搭配得当、使用方便、便于观赏，尽可能与台布、餐具的色调相结合。餐巾花在摆放时要注意以下几点：
(1) Distinguished arrangement
Napkin flowers are generally inserted into cups or placed on plates, so it is necessary to distinguish the methods. Napkin flowers with a larger bottom should be inserted into water cups, while those that need to be laid flat are suitable for placing on plates. The napkin flower should be inserted into the cup at a suitable depth, generally 2/3 of the depth of the cup. The exposed part is the ornamental part, so attention should be paid to the perfection of the pattern when inserting. Because the cup is transparent, the part in the cup cannot be ignored; it should be clear and away from random plugging. Napkin flowers placed on the plate should be straight and steady.
（一）区别插摆
餐巾花一般插入水杯或摆放在盘中，因此要区别插摆。底部较大的花型宜插入水杯中，需要平摆摆放的花形则适宜放置于盘里。餐巾花插入水杯中的深度要适当，一般撷在水杯深度的 2/3 为宜，露在外面的部分为观赏部分，因此插摆的时候应该要注意调整花型。由于杯子是透明的，因此杯子内的部分也不能忽视，应该线条清楚，忌乱掖乱塞。摆放在盘里的餐巾花要正、要稳。
(2) Properly matched
The varieties of napkin flower should be matched properly to highlight the host and other guests. The flower shapes at other guests' positions should be evenly high and low, and well-arranged. When placing napkin flowers on the same table, varieties and shapes that are similar, or heights and sizes that are similar, should be staggered and symmetrically placed; the same flower shape should generally not be placed together.
（二）搭配得当
餐巾花的品种要搭配得当，凸显主位，其他宾客位置的花形应高低均匀，错落有致。同桌摆放时，要将品种、形状相似的花形，或高低、大小相似的花形，错开并对称摆放，一般不宜将相同花型摆放在一起。
(3) Paying attention to the orientation
Pay attention to the orientation of napkin flowers. The view side should face the guests. Napkin flowers should be placed evenly and neatly, neither block tableware and other items on the table, nor affect service operation.
（三）注意朝向
餐巾花要注意摆放的朝向，应将观赏性的一面对着宾客席位。餐巾花摆放要注意距离匀称，整齐一致，不能挡住餐具和餐台上的其他用品，也不能影响服务操作。

4. Normal Napkin Flower Folding Style and Process

The following table shows common plate-flower styles and their folding steps. Each style is illustrated by step photographs in the textbook.

四、常见餐巾折花及折叠步骤

下表展示了常见的盘花造型及其折叠步骤，每个造型在教材中均配有分步照片。
表 3.7 餐巾花折叠步骤（盘花） Napkin Flower Folding Process (Plate Flowers)
名称Name步骤Process完成图Graphic Model
金字塔Pyramid 将餐巾对折成三角形，两侧角向中心折叠后立起，整理成金字塔状。Fold the napkin in half diagonally, fold the side corners toward the center, then stand it up to form a pyramid. 金字塔状盘花Pyramid-shaped plate flower.
济公帽（僧帽）Mitral 将餐巾四角反复向中心折叠，再向内收成帽状。Fold the four corners of the napkin toward the center repeatedly, then tuck inward to form a hat shape. 僧帽状盘花Mitral-shaped plate flower.
法国褶French Plait 将餐巾折成均匀的风琴褶，再折叠成紧凑的褶状造型。Fold the napkin into even accordion pleats and then fold it into a compact pleated shape. 褶状盘花Pleated plate flower.
帐篷Tent 将餐巾对角折叠，两侧角向中间合拢，整理成帐篷状。Fold the napkin diagonally and bring the side corners together to form a tent shape. 帐篷状盘花Tent-shaped plate flower.
蜗牛Snail 将餐巾卷折后盘成蜗牛壳状。Roll the napkin and then coil it into a snail-shell shape. 蜗牛状盘花Snail-shaped plate flower.
圆锥帽Conical Hat 将餐巾卷成圆锥形，翻出帽檐，整理成圆锥帽状。Roll the napkin into a cone, turn out the brim and shape into a conical hat. 圆锥帽状盘花Conical-hat-shaped plate flower.
星光灿烂Starry Night 将餐巾多次折叠后逐层展开，形成放射状花形。Fold the napkin into multiple layers and then fan it out into a radiating flower shape. 放射状盘花Radiating plate flower.
帆船Sailing Boat 将餐巾折出船身与船帆，整理成帆船状。Fold the napkin into a boat hull and add a sail. 帆船状盘花Sailing-boat-shaped plate flower.

拓展阅读Extended Reading

The Origin and Development of Napkins

Ancient Chinese classics record that "napkins" were used at banquets to cover food and wipe hands. During the Ming and Qing dynasties, high-grade brocade embroidered napkins appeared at court and noble banquets. It can be said that napkins are not entirely imported.

Western napkins have a long history. Ancient Greek and Roman nobles always maintained the habit of eating with their hands, so after meals they would use a towel-sized napkin to wipe their hands. More particular nobles would wash their hands in a finger bowl after wiping them. In addition to water, the finger bowl contained rose petals. Ancient Egyptians also put almonds, cinnamon and orange blossoms in the bowl. By the 17th century, besides being practical, the ornamental value of napkins was increasingly emphasized. In 1680, Italy already had more than 20 ways to fold napkins, such as Noah's Ark used by priests, hen shape used by ladies, and shapes liked by common people such as rooster, turtle, bull, bear, rabbit, etc., with various forms.

The modern napkin we use is a product combining Chinese and Western elements, widely used in various restaurant services, and has become an important part of restaurants and service.

1. What is the earliest recorded use of napkins in China?
To cover food and wipe hands at banquets.
2. What kind of napkins appeared at court and noble banquets during the Ming and Qing dynasties?
High-grade brocade embroidered napkins.
3. How did ancient Greek and Roman nobles use napkins?
They used towel-sized napkins to wipe their hands after eating.
4. What happened to napkin folding in 17th-century Italy?
It became an ornamental art, with more than 20 folding styles.
5. What is the modern napkin described as?
A product combining Chinese and Western elements.

项目四 摆台

Project 4 Table Setting
项目概述Project Overview
Table setting refers to the arrangement and placement of the tableware used by guests on the dining table. The overall table setting, besides the initial laying of the tablecloth and the final decoration of the table top, also includes the arrangement of tableware, flowers, the placement of condiments, etc. Different restaurants have different forms of table setting; however it is arranged, it should be based on safety, hygiene, rationality, neatness, uniformity and the convenience of service.
摆台，是指餐桌上供客人使用的餐具摆设放置的状况。整体的餐桌摆设，除了包括最初的台布铺设和最终桌面的装饰摆放之外，还包括餐具的排列、花卉、调味品的放置等。各餐厅有不同形态的摆台，不论如何摆放均应该以安全、卫生、合理、整齐、统一以及服务的便利性为出发点。
Whether a restaurant's table setting is reasonable and beautiful will greatly affect the guests' dining experience. Whether it is the choice of tablecloth material, the matching of colors, or the material and quality of the tableware and glassware, restaurant operators need to pay careful attention.
餐厅的摆台是否合理、美观将会极大地影响客人的用餐体验。无论是台布材质的选择、颜色的搭配，还是餐、酒具的材质与品质，都需要餐厅经营者用心关注。
Because of the differences in food culture, Chinese and Western dining differ greatly in the choice of cloth and the use of utensils. On the basis of understanding the differences in food culture, further discussing the principles and methods of Chinese and Western table setting can better reflect the quality of restaurant service.
因为饮食文化的差异，中、西餐所用在布料选择、器具使用上有着很大的不同。在理解饮食文化差异的基础上，再探讨中、西餐摆台的原则与方法，更能体现出餐厅服务的品质。
项目目标Project Objectives
项目目标 Project Objectives
类别Category内容Content
知识目标Knowledge Objectives了解中、西餐摆台的原则；掌握中、西餐摆台所需要的器具；掌握中、西餐摆台的步骤。Understand the principles of Chinese and Western table setting; master the utensils required for Chinese and Western table setting; master the steps of Chinese and Western table setting.
技能目标Skill Objectives熟练进行中、西餐台布的铺设，熟练进行中、西餐台面的摆设。Skillfully lay the tablecloth for Chinese and Western dining, and skillfully set the Chinese and Western table tops.
素质目标Quality Objectives能够依据实际情况进行中、西餐台面的设计与摆放。Be able to design and arrange the Chinese and Western table tops according to the actual situation.
思政目标Ideological Objectives具有强烈的民族自信与文化自信，具备精益求精的工匠精神；具有强烈的职业责任感，具备创新意识。Possess a strong national and cultural self-confidence, and have the craftsmanship spirit of striving for perfection; possess a strong sense of professional responsibility and an innovative consciousness.

任务1 中餐摆台

Task 1: Chinese Table Setting
案例导入Case Introduction
A guest came to the restaurant for a meal. After being seated, the guest noticed that all the napkins on the table had been freshly replaced with new ones, and the whole table looked clean and tidy. This small detail made the guest feel the restaurant's attentive service. Thinking and discussion: How should guest-use items in a restaurant be used and managed in a standardized way?
一位客人来到餐厅用餐。入座后，客人发现餐桌上的餐巾都换了新的，整个台面看起来干净整洁。这个小细节让客人感受到了餐厅用心的服务。思考讨论：餐厅客用物品如何规范使用？
知识准备Knowledge Preparation

1. Spread the Tablecloth

The tablecloth is one of the essential items for restaurant table setting. The choice of its specifications and color should be coordinated with the size of the table and the style of the restaurant.

一、铺台布

台布是餐厅摆台必备的物品之一。台布的规格及颜色的选择，应与餐台的大小、餐厅的风格协调一致。
(1) Category of the tablecloth
Among numerous kinds of tablecloth, cotton cloth is outstanding in water absorption, which is widely used in current restaurants. The patterns of tablecloth vary from single cluster, bunches, craft embroidery to decorative fabric, usually against a white background, which on most occasions is determined by the style, decoration and ambience of the restaurant. The shape of the cloth ranges from the square—which is frequently applied to square and round tables in Chinese setting—to rectangle and round ones, which go for round tables.
（一）台布的种类
台布的种类有很多，因纯棉材质吸水性能好，大多数餐厅广泛使用纯棉提花台布。台布的图案有团花、散花、工艺绣花及装饰布等。台布的颜色多种多样，但多数餐厅选用白色。台布的颜色要与餐厅的风格、装饰、环境相协调。通常来说，台布的形状有正方形、长方形和圆形三种。中餐摆台中，正方形常用于方台或圆台，圆形台布主要用于圆台。
(2) Size of the tablecloth
The size of tablecloth is determined by the size of the table. For example, a 140cm × 140cm cloth applies to a 90cm × 90cm square table; a 160cm × 160cm one corresponds to a 100cm × 100cm or 110cm × 110cm square table; a 200cm × 200cm tablecloth is suitable for a round table of 170cm diameter; a 220cm × 220cm one caters for a round table of 180cm or 200cm diameter. Normally, round-shaped tablecloths are customized: the diameter is made 60cm larger than that of the table, so that when laid, the cloth hangs about 30cm around the edge.
（二）台布的规格
正方形台布的规格有多种，应根据餐桌的大小选择适当规格的台布。如 140cm×140cm 的台布适用于 90cm×90cm 的方台；160cm×160cm 的台布适用于 100cm×100cm 或 110cm×110cm 的方台；200cm×200cm 的台布适用于直径 170cm 的圆台；220cm×220cm 的台布适用于直径 180cm 或 200cm 的圆台。圆形台布的规格各有不同，一般的圆形台布多需定制，即根据餐台的大小将台布制成直径大于餐台直径 60cm 的圆形台布，使台布铺于餐台上时圆周下垂 30cm 左右。
(3) Laying the tablecloth
Laying the tablecloth is a process to gently and evenly pave the cloth on the table. Before that, the server should put chairs around the table according to the number of diners, aligning them by twos and threes. Then the server should examine every cloth to make sure it is clean, without any loophole, stain or wrinkle. Also, place chairs according to the direction and design of the table. When performing the layout, the server should move out the chair of the deputy host first, then stand in its place, keep a distance of about 40cm to the side of the table, and put the chosen tablecloth on the deputy host's table.
（三）台布的铺设
铺台布是将台布平整、均匀地落在餐桌上的过程。铺台前，服务人员应根据就餐人数将餐椅摆放于餐台周围，两两、三三对齐；然后检查台布，确保台布干净、无破损、无污迹、无褶皱；将台布按餐台的设计方向摆放；操作时，应先拉出副主人的餐椅，然后站立在副主人餐椅处，距餐台约 40cm，将选好的台布放于副主人的餐台上。
(4) Three ways to spread the tablecloth

① Push-and-pull. Open the cloth with both hands, place it on the table, push it out parallel to the table and then pull it back. This method is commonly used in a la carte restaurants and smaller restaurants.

② Cast-net. Open the tablecloth parallelly on both sides, stand firmly with the right foot in front of the left. Lift the cloth to chest level, then turn the upper body to the left with feet unmoved; as the body turns back, cast the cloth diagonally forward. When it lands, the center of the cloth should overlap the center of the table. The motion should be natural and graceful. This method is used in spacious venues or skill competitions.

③ Shake. Open the tablecloth parallelly on both sides, stand upright, and use the power of the wrists to shake the cloth open in one motion and lay it flat on the table. This method suits venues with ample space.

（四）铺台布的三种方法

① 推拉式铺台。用双手将台布打开后放至餐台上，将台布沿着餐台平行推出去再拉回来。这种铺法多用于零点餐厅或较小的餐厅。

② 撒网式铺台。双手将台布平行打开，右脚在前、左脚在后站立，将台布横向提起与手臂平行，身体向左转，借助腕力将台布向前抛出；身体回转时，台布随着向前撤出。台布抛至前方时，上身转体回位并回复至正位站立，台布应平铺于餐台上，台布中心应与台面中心重合。抛撒时动作应自然、潇洒。这种铺法多用于宽大场地或技术比赛场合。

③ 抖铺式铺台。双手将台布平行打开，身体呈正位站立，利用双腕的力量，将台布向前一次性抖开并平铺于餐台上。这种铺台方法适合于较宽敞的场地。

The Operation Procedure of Laying Tablecloth in a Chinese Restaurant
  1. Stand at the deputy host's position.
  2. Hold two sides of the cloth and pull them apart as far as possible.
  3. Hold the tablecloth between thumb and forefinger; lift it with both hands after parallel folding.
  4. Fold the tablecloth in front of you with both hands and lean forward slightly.
  5. Shake and push: stand forward, shake the cloth and lay it on the table with the help of the wrist.
  6. As the cloth falls, slowly pull it to the table edge and near the body.
  7. Locate: adjust the center of the cloth to make sure its center overlaps with that of the table.
  8. The convex side of the cloth faces upward, with the centerline pointing directly toward the seats of the host and deputy host.
  9. Set the turntable and make sure the spindle is in the middle of the table.
中餐铺台布操作流程
  1. 站立在副主人位置。
  2. 双手持台布两侧，将台布平行打开，尽量拉开。
  3. 用拇指和食指捏住台布，双手提拿。
  4. 双手将台布向前折叠，身体前倾。
  5. 抖、推：身体呈正位站立，利用双腕的力量将台布向前抖开并推铺于餐台。
  6. 台布下落时缓慢把台布拉至桌子边沿和近身体处。
  7. 台布定位：调整台布中心，使其与餐桌中心重合。
  8. 台布正面凸面朝上，中心线直对正、副主人席位。
  9. 放转盘：把转盘放在转轴上，转轴在桌子正中。
(5) Notices
When spreading the tablecloth, it should be kept away from the ground. The intersection of the creases should overlap with the center of the table, and the convex side of the cloth should face upward with the centerline pointing directly toward the seat of the host in front and the deputy host at the back. The server should make sure the cloth has no wrinkles on its surface, with its four corners hanging evenly and at equal distance from the ground; after laying, the pulled-out chair should be returned to its place. The server should pay attention to hand strength when spreading the tablecloth: do not pull with too much strength; do not pull too gently; keep even strength on both sides.
（五）注意事项
铺台布时，台布不能接触地面，台布中间折痕的交叉点应正好在餐台的中心处；台布的正面凸面朝上、中心线直对正、副主人席位；四角呈直线下垂状，下垂部分距地面距离相等；铺好的台布应平整无褶皱。铺好台布后，应将拉出的餐椅送回原位。还应注意铺台布时的手部力量：避免拉台布力量过大，避免推台布力量不够，避免两手用力不均。

2. Table Setting

Table setting is the process of putting the tableware on the table.

二、摆台

摆台是将餐具摆放于餐桌上的过程。
(1) The Procedure and Standard of Chinese Banquet Table Setting

Position: Stand on the seat of the deputy host, which is on the right side of the host's seat.

Methods: Push-and-pull, shake, or cast-net.

Standard: Put the tablecloth upward on the table and finish on the first try if possible; make sure the center of the cloth is in the middle of the table with the convex side pointing toward the host's and deputy host's seats; make sure the four edges of the cloth hang evenly to the ground, the surface parallel with the legs of the table and perpendicular to the ground.

Arrange seats: For round tables, place seats in a "three-three-two-two" form; for square tables, place seats in a symmetrical "two-two-one-one" form, with seats facing the center of the table starting from the host's seat, at equal distance and about 1.5cm from the table.

Set the turntable: For groups of more than eight people, set a turntable concentric with the table; check that it rotates smoothly.

Set the centerpiece: Choose a piece whose specifications suit the table; it should be beautiful, with strong ornamental value, accord with the banquet theme, and play the role of foiling and embellishing the theme.

Set the tableware: Use a tray, distribute the wares clockwise from the host's seat, handle them gently and pay attention to sanitation.

（一）中餐宴会摆台的程序与标准

位置：站在主人位右侧的副主人位。

方法：推拉式、抖铺式、撒网式。

标准：台布正面朝上，一次铺成；台布中心在餐桌中心，凸面朝向正、副主人席位；台布四角下垂均匀，台面平整、与桌腿垂直、与地面平行。

摆餐椅：圆桌摆放呈"三三两两"式，方桌摆放呈"两两一一"式或"对称式"，从主位开始，餐椅正对餐位，椅子之间距离相等，与餐桌相距约 1.5cm。

放转盘：8 人以上摆放转盘，与餐台同心，注意检查转盘旋转是否顺畅。

摆放中心装饰物：装饰物规格与餐桌外形相适当，美观大方，起到烘托和点缀主题的作用，并应符合宴会主题。

摆餐具：使用托盘操作，从主人位开始、按顺时针方向依次摆放；摆放时要求轻放，注意卫生标准。

Tableware placement details (Chinese banquet)
  • Locate the plate: Hold the edge of the plate and place it at the center of each seat, aligned with the seat center, spaced equally, about 1.5cm from the table edge. If a pattern or logo is present, place it uniformly.
  • Soup bowl, spoon and sauce dish: Place the sauce dish in front of the plate, 1cm from its nearest edge; place the soup bowl on the top-left of the plate, 1cm from the sauce dish; put the spoon in the bowl with its handle pointing left; keep the three items horizontally level.
  • Chopsticks rack, chopsticks and toothpick: Place the rack to the right of the plate at its top third; set chopsticks and ladle on the rack, 3cm from the plate and 1.5cm from the table edge, equal distance from the edge; pattern/logo face front; lay the toothpick between chopsticks and ladle, front side up, end aligned with the ladle handle.
  • Public tableware: Serving chopsticks rack, spoon and chopsticks in front of the host's and deputy host's seats, 5–10cm from the turntable; serving chopsticks inside, serving spoon outside, handle to the right.
  • Water glass, wine glass and liquor glass: Wine glass in front of the sauce dish, liquor glass to its right, water glass to its left, 1cm apart, on the same line at 30° to the horizontal. If cup flowers are folded, place the water glass after the flowers are done.
  • Napkin flower: Chinese banquets may use cup flowers or plate flowers; they should be placed properly, beautifully and dignified.

Double check: After finishing, check that the quantity and quality of the setting meet the standard.

餐具摆放细则（中餐宴会）
  • 摆餐盘（定位盘）：手持餐盘边缘，放于餐位正中，与餐位中心对齐，间距相等，距台边约 1.5cm；如有图案或店徽，应摆放在统一位置。
  • 摆汤碗、汤勺和味碟：味碟放在餐盘正前方，距餐盘最近边 1cm；汤碗放在餐盘左上方，距味碟 1cm；汤勺放在汤碗中，勺柄朝左；三者保持水平一线。
  • 摆筷架、筷子和牙签：筷架放在餐盘右侧上方三分之一处；筷子、汤勺（勺托）放在筷架上，筷尾距餐盘 3cm，距台边 1.5cm；筷子与台边距离相等；图案或店徽正面朝前；牙签放在筷子和筷子之间，正面朝上，末端与勺柄对齐。
  • 摆公用餐具：公筷架、公勺、公筷放在正、副主人位前方，距转盘 5~10cm，公勺在里、公筷在外、勺柄朝右。
  • 摆水杯、葡萄酒杯和白酒杯：葡萄酒杯放在味碟正上方，白酒杯在其右侧，水杯在左侧，三杯相距 1cm，在同一水平线上，与水平线呈 30°；若折杯花，水杯待餐巾花做完后再放。
  • 摆餐巾花：中餐宴会可以摆设杯花，也可摆放盘花，应摆放得当、美观大方。

复查：摆放完毕后，检查并确认摆台的数量与质量是否符合标准。

(2) The Procedure and Standard of A-la-carte Service Table Setting
  • Prepare: Prepare clean, undamaged tableware; keep hands clean; check that the table and chairs are clean and complete.
  • Position: Stand on the deputy host's seat, to the right of the host's.
  • Standard: Tablecloth upward on the table; its center in the middle of the table, the surface hanging parallel to the legs and perpendicular to the ground, whether square or round. The cloth should be flat and neat without crimp damage; set the table after the cloth is laid.
  • Set tableware: Use a tray, distribute wares clockwise from the host's seat, place gently and mind sanitation.

Details: Locate the plate at the seat center with pattern/logo uniform; soup bowl on the top-left of the plate with spoon handle left; chopsticks 3cm right of the plate, pattern up, 1.5cm from the edge; wine glasses on the top-right, water glass left and wine glass right, their diameters on one line, the wine-glass bottom aligned with the chopsticks; seasoning bottles, vases and napkin flowers placed in order, standing elegantly and appropriately.

（二）中餐零点摆台的程序与标准
  • 准备：准备干净、无破损的餐具；服务人员摆台前保持双手清洁；检查餐桌、餐椅是否洁净、完好。
  • 位置：站在副主人位，即主人位的右侧。
  • 标准：台布正面朝上；台布中心在餐桌中心，台面平整，与桌腿平行、与地面垂直，无论方台或圆台；台布应平整、无褶皱，铺好台布后再摆台。
  • 摆餐具：使用托盘操作，从主人位开始按顺时针方向依次摆放，要求轻放，注意卫生标准。

细则：摆餐盘（定位盘）于餐位正中，图案店徽统一；汤碗放在餐盘左上方，汤勺勺柄朝左；筷子放在餐盘右侧，距餐盘 3cm，图案向上，距台边 1.5cm；酒杯放在餐盘右上方，水杯在左、酒杯在右，三者口径在同一直线上，酒杯底部与筷子对齐；调味瓶、花瓶等摆放有序、得当、美观大方，餐巾花造型得体。

任务实施Task Implementation

Step 1: The teacher introduces the knowledge of Chinese table setting, and arranges the Chinese training room into two areas: the a la carte area and the banquet (private room) area.

Step 2: The teacher demonstrates the laying of the tablecloth and the Chinese a la carte and banquet table setting, and reminds students of matters needing attention.

Step 3: Work in groups (5 persons per group) to complete the Chinese a la carte and banquet table-setting exercises according to the steps demonstrated by the teacher. Two groups of students do exercises in the two areas at the same time (five students take turns to set the table).

Step 4: Select one student from each group to perform the Chinese banquet table setting, and then the teacher evaluates and summarizes the settings.

第一步：教师介绍中餐摆台的相关知识。将中餐实训室布置成 2 个区域：零点区域和宴会（包间）区域。

第二步：教师演示台布的铺设、中餐零点及宴会的摆台，并提醒学生相关注意事项。

第三步：以小组（5 人 1 组）为单位，根据教师演示步骤，完成中餐零点和宴会摆台练习。2 组同学在 2 个区域同时进行（5 位学生轮流进行摆台练习）。

第四步：以小组为单位，各抽取一名同学进行中餐宴会摆台考核，并进行评价和总结。

评分标准（按 100 分制）：81—100 分为"优秀"；71—80 分为"良好"；60—70 分为"合格"；60 分以下为"不合格"。

拓展阅读Extended Reading

G20 Hangzhou Summit Tableware

In 2016, the G20 Summit was held in Hangzhou, Zhejiang. For the welcome state banquet prepared by China for the leaders of the G20, the design team of the G20 state-banquet tableware series came from Shanghai; their design inspiration was drawn from water and the natural landscape. The whole set of tableware reflected the keynote of "West Lake elements, Zhejiang characteristics, Jiangnan flavor, Chinese style, and the stature of a world power."

Figure 4.1 shows the G20 "West Lake" tableware. The graphic pattern adopted the aesthetic of traditional culture, with careful and precise craftsmanship; the shapes of the lotus, the lotus seedpod and the kettle lid were all integrated into the design.

Figure 4.2 shows the G20 Hangzhou Summit tableware. The bridge-shaped outline was also integrated into the shape of the appliances. The cold-dish platter was modeled on "Three Pools Mirroring the Moon," one of the ten scenic spots of West Lake; another dish drew on "Sweet Osmanthus Rain at Manjuelong Village," using the osmanthus—the city flower of Hangzhou—and Jiangnan imagery.

Figure 4.3 shows further pieces of the G20 Hangzhou Summit tableware.

The Welcome Dinner Menu of the G20 Hangzhou Summit

  • Cold Dishes / Hors d'Oeuvres
  • Pine Mushroom Soup
  • Sweet and Sour Mandarin Fish with Pine Nuts
  • Stir-Fried Shrimps with Longjing Tea Leaves
  • Orange-Flavored Crab Meat
  • Hangzhou-Style Fillet Steak
  • Braised Vegetables
  • Pastries
  • Fruits and Icecream
  • Coffee and Tea
  • Red Wine Changyu 2012, Beijing China
  • White Wine Changyu 2011, Beijing China
1. Where and when was the G20 Summit held, according to the passage?
It was held in Hangzhou, Zhejiang, China, in 2016.
2. What was the design inspiration for the G20 state-banquet tableware series?
The inspiration came from water and natural landscape.
3. Which city flower of Hangzhou is referenced in the tableware design?
The osmanthus (桂花), which is the city flower of Hangzhou.
4. Which West Lake scenic spot inspired the shape of the cold-dish platter?
"Three Pools Mirroring the Moon" (三潭印月), one of the ten scenic spots of West Lake.
5. Name two dishes from the G20 Hangzhou Summit welcome dinner menu.
Examples: Sweet and Sour Mandarin Fish with Pine Nuts; Stir-Fried Shrimps with Longjing Tea Leaves (any two from the menu are acceptable).

任务2 西餐摆台

Task 2: Western Table Setting
案例导入Case Introduction
A high-end Western restaurant attached great importance to a VIP guest's visit. The restaurant carefully designed its table setting—from the choice of tablecloth and the arrangement of glassware to the placement of each piece of cutlery—so that the whole table presented a unique and elegant style. The VIP was deeply impressed and later left a thank-you letter. Thinking and discussion: What is unique about this Western restaurant's table design?
一家西餐厅高度重视此次 VIP 客人的到来。餐厅精心设计了餐台——从台布的选择、酒具的摆放到每一件餐具的放置，使整个台面呈现出独特而优雅的风格。这位 VIP 客人深受感动，之后留下了感谢信。思考讨论：该西餐厅的餐台设计有何独特之处？
知识准备Knowledge Preparation

1. Tablecloth Laying

Before laying the tablecloth, the server should do the following inspections: (1) Whether the chosen tablecloth is correct; (2) Whether the desktop is clean; (3) Whether the table is stable.

一、台布铺设

台布铺设之前，服务人员要先做如下检查工作：(1) 选取的台布是否正确；(2) 桌面是否洁净；(3) 餐桌是否平稳。
The Operation Procedure of Laying Tablecloth in a Western Restaurant
  • Stand beside the table, with the folded edge of the tablecloth toward your right side, and open the cloth.
  • Hold the top tier of the cloth between thumb and forefinger, and hold the middle tier with the forefinger and middle finger.
  • After the dining chairs are placed, the center crease of the tablecloth is aligned to the center of the dining chairs; the center of the cloth crease is in the middle of the dining table, hanging about 30cm.
  • Make sure the cloth is clamped between the index and middle fingers and pulled to the table edge; bend the body slightly, one leg forward and one leg back; throw the tablecloth and gently pull it back until it covers the entire table.
西餐台布铺设操作流程
  • 站立于餐桌旁，将台布折叠边朝向自己右侧，打开台布。
  • 用拇指和食指夹住台布最上层，食指与中指夹住中间单层台布。
  • 放置餐椅后，台布折痕的中心点与餐椅中心对齐；台布折痕的中心在餐桌正中央，下垂约 30cm。
  • 确保台布夹在食指与中指之间并拉至桌边；身体微屈，一脚前一脚后；抛出台布并轻轻拉回，直至覆盖整个台面。

2. Principles of Western Table Setting

Generally, Western table setting is more diverse than Chinese. Western dining rooms vary, so service and table setting differ accordingly. Western table setting is mainly arranged according to the positioning of the restaurant and the dishes ordered by guests. Besides service convenience and usability, safety, hygiene and tidiness are also factors to consider.

二、西餐摆台的原则

一般来说，西餐摆台较中餐摆台更加多样。西餐厅种类繁多，服务方式及其台面摆设也不尽相同。西餐摆台的主要依据是餐厅的定位及客人所点菜品。除了服务及使用便利之外，安全卫生及整齐一致也是摆台时需要考虑的因素。

The principles of Western restaurant table setting are as follows:

  1. Tableware should be positioned on the display plate or table napkin, and placed in order from inside to outside. The spoon is placed on the right side, usually outside the main knife.
  2. Place the knife on the right side with the blade inward.
  3. Place the fork on the left side with the prong upward.
  4. The main knife and fork are placed next to the display plate, on the innermost side. The rest of the tableware is placed outward in order of serving, and the one to be used first is placed at the outermost side.
  5. The bread and butter dishes are placed on the left side. The center of the bread dish is aligned to the center of the decorative dish; the left edge of the butter dish is in line with the center of the bread dish.
  6. The butter knife is placed across the bread plate, grip facing right with blade downward, or on the right side of the plate with blade facing left.
  7. The dessert fork and spoon are placed horizontally on the upper side. The handle of the dessert fork faces left, next to the display plate; the handle of the dessert spoon faces right, on the dessert fork.
  8. The water cup is placed directly above the main knife, and the wine cups are arranged in a straight line on the lower right side of the water cup.
  9. Coffee spoons are not pre-set on the table.

General Western restaurants place only basic tableware in advance; after the guest orders, the server adds or removes tableware according to the order. According to European tradition, basic tableware includes: cloth, knife and fork, bread plate, butter knife, water glass, red wine cup, and pepper-and-salt shaker. Usually a display plate is seen in high-end Western restaurants, with the napkin also placed on it; in many informal Western restaurants only the napkin and the main-course knife and fork are found.

西餐摆台的原则如下：

  1. 餐具应放在展示盘或餐巾上，由内向外按顺序摆放；勺放在右侧，通常在主菜刀外侧。
  2. 刀类摆放在右侧，刀刃向内。
  3. 叉类摆放在左侧，叉齿向上。
  4. 主刀、主叉紧挨展示盘，在其内侧；其余餐具按上菜顺序由内向外摆放，最先使用的摆放在最外侧。
  5. 面包盘、黄油盘放在左侧，面包盘中心与装饰盘中心对齐，黄油盘左边与面包盘中心成一直线。
  6. 黄油刀放在面包盘上，横放，刀柄朝右、刀刃向下，或放在盘右侧、刀刃朝左。
  7. 甜品叉、勺放于上方，甜品叉握把朝左，紧邻展示盘；甜品勺握把朝右，置于甜品叉上。
  8. 水杯放在主刀正上方，酒杯在水杯右下方成一直线。
  9. 咖啡勺不预先摆在桌上。

一般的西餐厅，无论是客人单点还是吃套餐，事先都是只摆放基本餐具，待客人开始点菜后，服务人员可根据所点菜肴增减餐具。依照欧洲的传统，基本的餐具包括：餐布、刀叉、面包盘、黄油刀、水杯、红酒杯、椒盐瓶。通常，在高级西餐厅还会看到展示盘，口布也是置于展示盘上。在许多非正式的西餐厅，仅能看到口布和主菜刀叉。

3. The Table Settings of Western Breakfast

Western breakfast is mainly divided into continental breakfast and American breakfast.

Continental breakfast is a simple meal consisting of bread, jam, cream, coffee or black tea. The tableware needed is plate, knife, fork, coffee cup set, table napkin, salt and pepper shaker, etc.

American breakfast is richer, including meat, eggs, cheese, bread, fruit, coffee or tea. The tableware needed is plate, knife, fork, bread plate, butter knife, coffee cup set, table napkin, salt and pepper shaker, etc.

三、西式早餐摆台

西式早餐主要分为欧陆式早餐和美式早餐。

欧陆式早餐：供应餐点较为简单，包括面包、果酱、奶油、咖啡或红茶。所需餐具有：餐盘、刀、叉、咖啡杯具、餐巾、椒盐瓶等。

美式早餐：供应餐点较为丰富，包括肉、蛋、奶酪、面包、水果、咖啡或茶。所需餐具有：餐盘、刀、叉、面包盘、黄油刀、咖啡杯具、餐巾、椒盐瓶等。

4. The Table Settings of Western Lunch and Dinner

Generally, a restaurant only puts basic tableware in advance; according to the guest's order, the server adds or removes tableware. We list the basic settings of Western lunch and dinner, the basic a la carte order and the set-menu settings.

四、西式午、晚餐摆台

一般而言，西餐厅只预先摆放基本餐具，再根据客人点餐及需要更换及增减所要的餐具。下面我们将列举基本午、晚餐摆台，基本单点摆台及套餐摆台。
The Table Settings of Western Lunch and Dinner
西式午、晚餐摆台用品清单
西式午、晚餐摆台用品清单 Tableware List for Western Lunch & Dinner Settings

单点甜品 Single dessertSingle dessert甜品叉勺、餐巾、水杯、椒盐瓶Dessert fork and spoon, table napkin, water glass, salt and pepper shaker

5. The Table Settings of Western Banquets

The table setting of Western banquets follows these procedures and principles:
  1. Place the display plate and table napkin at the center of each seat, about 2cm from the table edge.
  2. Place the main knife on the right side of the plate, handle aligned to the plate, blade facing left.
  3. Place the spoon on the right side of the knife, its bottom aligned to the plate.
  4. Place the salad knife on the right side of the spoon, its bottom aligned to the plate.
  5. Place the main fork on the left side of the plate, its bottom aligned to the plate, prongs facing left.
  6. Place the salad fork on the left side of the main fork, bottom in line with the plate.
  7. Place the bread plate on the left side of the salad fork, its bottom aligned to the bottom of the display plate.
  8. Place the butter knife on the right side of the bread plate, 2–3cm from its right edge.
  9. Place the dessert fork and spoon above the main plate, dessert-fork handle left, dessert-spoon handle right.
  10. Place the water glass 2–3cm above the knife.
  11. Place the wine glass in a straight line at a 45° angle below the right of the water glass.
  12. Place vases and the salt-and-pepper shaker at the center of the table.

五、西式宴会摆台

西式宴会摆台需遵循以下程序与原则：
  1. 摆放展示盘和餐巾于每位餐位中心，距台边约 2cm。
  2. 将主菜刀放在餐盘右侧，刀柄与餐盘对齐，刀刃朝左。
  3. 将汤勺放在主菜刀右侧，勺底与餐盘平齐。
  4. 将沙拉刀放在汤勺右侧，勺底与餐盘对齐。
  5. 将主菜叉放在餐盘左侧，叉底与餐盘平齐，叉齿向左。
  6. 将沙拉叉放在主菜叉左侧，底部与餐盘成一直线。
  7. 将面包盘放在沙拉叉左侧，底部与展示盘底部对齐。
  8. 将黄油刀放在面包盘右侧，距面包盘右边 2–3cm。
  9. 将甜品叉、勺放在主菜盘上方，甜品叉柄朝左，甜品勺柄朝右。
  10. 把放水杯于餐刀正上方 2–3cm 处。
  11. 把红酒杯放在水杯右下方，成 45° 直线。
  12. 把花瓶、椒盐瓶放在餐桌中央。
任务实施Task Implementation

Step 1: The teacher introduces the relevant knowledge of Western restaurant table setting.

Step 2: The teacher demonstrates the laying of the table cover and the table setting of Western breakfast, lunch, dinner and banquet, and reminds students of matters needing attention.

Step 3: Taking the group (5 people per group) as the unit, complete the table-setting practice of Western breakfast, lunch, dinner and banquet according to the teacher's demonstration steps (5 students take turns in the setting exercises).

Step 4: Take one student from every group to be examined on the Western banquet table setting.

第一步：教师首先介绍西餐摆台的相关知识。

第二步：教师演示西餐摆台的台布铺设，西餐早、午、晚餐及西式宴会的摆台，并提醒学生相关注意事项。

第三步：以小组（5 人 1 组）为单位，根据教师演示步骤，完成西餐的早、午、晚餐及西式宴会摆台练习（5 位学生轮流进行摆台练习）。

第四步：以小组为单位，各抽取一名同学进行西餐宴会摆台考核。

拓展阅读Extended Reading

The Origin of Western Tableware

When we eat Western food, we use Western tableware. Western tableware is highly diverse, and for those who have rarely eaten Western food, the array of large and small utensils can be a real headache. Yet it is precisely because of these utensils that eating Western food feels like a refined and enjoyable experience. Every piece of Western tableware has its own distinct origin.

The Western fork first appeared in the 11th century in Tuscany, Italy, with only two prongs at first. The clergy strongly resisted the fork, believing that humans could only touch God's food with their hands, and that Tuscany's invention of the fork was inspired by Satan—an act of blasphemy. Italian historical records note that a Venetian died within days of eating with a fork; in fact he likely died of the plague, but the clergy believed otherwise. They often even held the fork in hand as a dueling weapon.

For 14th-century English people, the fork was still an exotic import. Most Europeans preferred to cut food with a knife and eat it with their hands. If a man ate with a fork, it meant he was not a "real man." Later, however, the fork gradually became a symbol of status, luxury and refinement, and eventually an essential utensil.

The table knife occupied an important place in human life very early. Millions of years ago, human ancestors began to use stone knives as tools. The knife at the waist was used not only for cutting meat but also for defense. Only those of status and rank... A certain cardinal made a contribution in this regard; even for ordinary life details, this bishop left a profound influence. At that time the tip of the table knife was not the familiar oval shape but had a sharp blade. Many bureaucrats used the table knife as a weapon after meals. Dissatisfied with this phenomenon, the cardinal ordered his servants to grind the knife blade into a round shape, so that it could no longer be used to stab.

The spoon also has a long history. As early as the Stone Age, spoons appeared in Asia. In ancient Egyptian tombs, spoons made of wood, stone, ivory and pottery have been found. Many silver spoons produced in Europe were mostly given as gifts.

1. When and where did the Western fork first appear?
It first appeared in the 11th century in Tuscany, Italy.
2. Why did the clergy initially resist the use of forks?
They believed humans should touch God's food only with their hands, and saw the fork as inspired by Satan—a blasphemy.
3. How did the cardinal change the design of the table knife, and why?
He ordered the sharp blade to be ground into a round shape so the knife could no longer be used as a weapon after meals.
4. According to the passage, what did the fork eventually become a symbol of?
A symbol of status, luxury and refinement, and then an essential utensil.
5. What materials were ancient Egyptian spoons made from, as mentioned in the text?
Wood, stone, ivory and pottery.

项目五 点餐服务

Project 5: Order Service
项目概述Project Overview

With the advent of the information age, informatization has become an inevitable trend in the development of restaurants. Today, more and more restaurants and hotels are paying attention to F&B ordering systems, and are gradually using ordering software to replace manual management. Driven by the mobile Internet, electronic ordering systems will become one of the keys to success for restaurants and hotels in the information age.

However, an ordering system is only a tool to improve work efficiency, reduce ordering mistakes and enhance standardized management. If a restaurant wants to provide unique, high-quality service to its guests, the presence of human order-taking is still necessary.

Order service is a basic skill that restaurant waiters must possess. Because guests differ in their dietary preferences, habits and methods, as well as in their familiarity with the products offered, their favored flavors and price requirements, an order-taker who only masters basic service skills is not enough; he or she must also learn to flexibly promote dishes according to different people, handle emergencies, and bring maximum benefit to the restaurant.

The order-taker's familiarity with the menu and knowledge of the dishes will affect the guests' overall dining evaluation. The level of ordering service is directly related to customer satisfaction as well as the hotel's profit and reputation; therefore, doing a good job of hotel ordering service is a very important task.

Order service tests employees' professional knowledge and skills, self-management skills and transferable skills. It examines the employee's familiarity with the menu, knowledge of the cooking techniques of dishes, communication skills with guests, guiding ability and self-management level.

随着信息时代的到来，信息化成为餐厅发展的必然趋势之一。如今，越来越多的餐厅和酒店开始关注餐饮点菜系统，并逐渐使用餐饮点菜软件取代人工管理。在移动互联网的推动下，点菜系统电子化将成为餐厅和酒店在信息时代制胜的秘诀之一。

然而，点餐系统只是提高餐厅工作效率、减少点餐失误、提升餐厅规范管理的工具，餐厅要想为客人提供独一无二的优质服务，还需要人工点餐的存在。

点餐服务是餐厅服务员必须具备的基本技能。由于客人的饮食喜好、饮食习惯、饮食方法不同，以及对于餐厅所供应产品的熟悉程度、风味、价格等要求不同，因此点餐员只掌握基本的服务技能是不够的，还要学会因人而异地灵活推销菜品、应对突发状况，为餐厅带来最大化收益。

点餐员对菜单的熟悉程度以及对菜品的了解程度，会影响客人的整体用餐评价。点菜服务水准的高低，直接关系到顾客满意度，以及酒店效益和声誉，因此做好酒店点菜服务是一项非常重要的工作。

点餐服务考查的是员工的专业知识技能、自我管理技能和可迁移技能，检测的是员工对菜单的熟悉程度、对菜品烹调工艺的了解、对客沟通能力、引导能力以及自我管理水平。

项目目标Project Objectives
项目目标 Project Objectives
类别Category内容Content
知识目标Knowledge掌握点菜所需要的基础知识及点菜的基本流程。Master the basic knowledge required for ordering and the basic ordering procedures.
技能目标Skill掌握点菜服务的方法；灵活运用点菜推销技巧。Master the methods of order service; flexibly apply dish-selling techniques.
素质目标Quality通过观察与沟通，能够为客人推荐合适的菜品。Through observation and communication, be able to recommend suitable dishes to guests.
思政目标Ideological具备民族自信；具备节约意识；具备服务意识与安全意识，并不断突破自我、勇于创新的意识。Possess national self-confidence; possess a sense of economy; possess a sense of service and safety, together with an awareness of constantly breaking through oneself and daring to innovate.

任务一 点菜服务

Task 1: Taking Orders (Chinese Restaurant)
案例导入Case Introduction

One weekend in May, ten guests arrived at Room 203 of a certain hotel. At first, the guests ordered a standard set meal priced at 380 yuan. The waiter said, "All right, we can add more dishes later if it is not enough." During the meal, the guests added two home-style dishes. However, because the waiter did not tell the guests that the extra dishes would be charged separately, when the guests looked at the bill they asked, "Waiter, why are these two dishes charged extra?" The waiter immediately explained to the guests that these two dishes were not included in the set meal they had ordered. The guests were very dissatisfied. When settling the bill, the waiter reported the incident to the supervisor, who immediately took a fruit platter and went to the room to apologize to the guests. The guests were quite nice about it and said, "Young lady, you must explain things clearly in the future, or we won't pay the bill."

Thinking & Discussion: How should the waiter have explained the extra charges for added dishes when the guests ordered the set meal?

某年5月的一个周末，某饭店203房间来了10位客人。一开始，客人点的是380元的标准套餐。服务员说："可以的，不够吃的时候再给加菜。"用餐过程中，客人加了两个家常菜。但因为服务员没有告诉客人加菜需要另付费用，所以当客人在看账单时就问道："服务员，怎么这两个菜加钱了呢？"服务员立刻向客人解释，这两道菜不在客人点的套餐内。客人很不满意。当给客人结账时，服务员将这件事情告诉了主管，主管立刻拿了一份果盘，去房间里给客人道歉，客人的态度也很好，说："小姑娘，以后一定要说明白了，不然就不给你们结账了。"

思考讨论：如果你是这名服务员，在客人点套餐时你会如何说明加菜的费用问题？

知识准备Knowledge Preparation

1. Preparation before taking the order

  1. Be familiar with the recipe, cooking time, flavor characteristics and the requirements of dishing up of the cuisine on the menu.
  2. Be familiar with the units on the menu, such as the quantity of the cuisine. The size of the plate, weight or the number are usually used as the unit.
  3. Master the dietary habits and dishes of the guests from different countries and regions, and be familiar with guests' taste and dietary requirements, which could help give better recommendations to the guests.
  4. Master the components and amounts of dishes required by different groups of guests.
  5. Master the order sequence, timing and seasonings.

2. Service procedures of taking orders in a Chinese restaurant

With regard to the form, the service procedures of taking orders in a Chinese restaurant seem rather simple, which include delivering the menu, greeting the guest, taking the order, introducing and recommending dishes, recording dishes, and repeating and confirming.

中餐点菜服务流程与标准 The Procedures and Standards of Ordering Service in a Chinese Restaurant
序号 No.服务环节 Procedure服务标准与要求 Standard & Requirement
1递送菜单
Deliver the menu		Generally the hostess or waiter delivers the menu to the guest. If the restaurant takes the order through a mobile phone or iPad, the waiter should instruct the guest how to take the order if they need.
2问候客人
Greet the guest		Greet the guest, then ask if the guest is ready to order, e.g. "May I have your order, please?"
3询问客人是否可以点菜
Ask if ready to order		Stand on the left side of the guest, lean forward slightly and listen carefully. Politely say "Please take your time," then gently ask "May I have your order now?"
4点菜
Take the order		Be patient while the guest is reading the menu (or ordering by mobile/tablet), and answer questions in as much detail as possible; explain the material, taste and method of the dishes patiently. Follow the ordering sequence: cold dish before hot dish; meat or fish before vegetable; special/local dish before ordinary dish; dishes before soup, dessert and drink.
5介绍推荐
Introduce & recommend		A waiter should be considerate of the needs of the guest, ask about the guest's preferences and meat, and reasonably recommend the restaurant's special and signature dishes.
6记录菜名
Record the dishes		Clearly and accurately record the dishes ordered by different guests to avoid confusion.
7复述确认
Repeat & confirm		Repeat the food ordered by the guest and let the guest confirm it; ask about any drinks and record them; take the menu back after taking the order and tell the guest the waiting time.
8迅速下单
Place the order quickly		Place the order quickly. The order written down should be complete; make sure dishes are separated from wine and cold dishes from hot ones. The electronic ordering system used by some restaurants will automatically send the order to the relevant departments (kitchen and preparation) in time, which is more convenient and faster.

Notes: If a dish ordered by the guest is sold out, apologize and say "Sorry, we haven't any…", then recommend another: "Would you like to try…? It's delicious." If a dish takes a long time to prepare, explain it to the guest: "I'm afraid it will take some time to prepare, would you mind it?" Pay attention to body posture and do not place the order sheet on the table.

3. The methods of dish-ordering service

When taking orders for the guests, the waiter should master the method and be able to handle problems flexibly. The set of ordering methods is as follows:

(1) Regular ordering method (常规点菜法) — This method refers to the order process of cold dishes, hot dishes, drinks and staple food.

(2) Recommendation method (推荐点菜法) — This method is to recommend the restaurant's signature dishes, specialties, seasonal dishes, innovative dishes, etc.

(3) Sales promotion method (推销点菜法) — This method refers to promoting dishes according to the guests' consumption motivation:

  • Luncheonette (便餐): Diners come for various reasons — business trips, travel, study nearby, or nearby residents with special needs. They demand affordable options with not too much variety but fast serving speed, so the waiter should recommend high-quality, inexpensive dishes with short cooking time.
  • Attracted by a famous dish (慕名而来): Guests are attracted to a restaurant by its famous dish. The waiter should pay attention to introducing some special dishes which are few but of great quality.
  • Fete / Banquet (宴请): In the case of business, marriage or birthday feasts, some guests pay attention to ostentation; therefore the waiter should emphasize the delicacy of the dishes when recommending.
  • Party with friends or colleagues (朋友/同事聚会): Guests usually ask for a warm atmosphere in which they can talk while eating, so they do not demand too much of the dishes. Generally, dishes that are numerous in variety but not excessive, delicate but not too expensive, are preferred.

(4) Psychological method (心理点菜法) — This method promotes dishes according to the guests' characteristics:

  • Show-off type (炫耀型): This type of guest is emotional, concerned about friendship and face-saving, and loves to show off wealth, so they ask for good quality but not fast service.
  • Blank type (茫然型): This type of guest may seldom dine out; they do not know which restaurant is good or what they want to eat, and lack knowledge and experience.
  • Habitual type (习惯型): This type of guest prefers the flavor of a particular restaurant, a chef's reputation, or one or several dishes. When serving such guests, pay attention to greeting them and ask: "Mr. X, would you like the same dish as last time, or would you like to order something else? We have a new dish which you have never tasted before. Would you like to try it?"

4. The language skills of selling dishes

(1) Use selective questions (选择式问句): It is easier for the guests to make decisions by asking them in a selective promoting language. For example, the waiter could ask "Would you like black tea or green tea?" rather than "What kind of tea would you like?"

(2) Addition of language (语言的加法): Introducing the advantages of the dishes to the guests can make them interested. For example: "This dish is not only delicious, but the ingredients are very fresh and contain a variety of vitamins. In addition, it has an auxiliary curative effect on deficiency-fire and other conditions."

(3) Subtraction of language (语言的减法): Explain the characteristics of the restaurant's specials and tell the guest it would be a pity if they didn't taste it. For example: "This kind of whitebait is only found in the waters around Lake Taihu. If you don't taste it now, it will be difficult for you to taste it when you return home."

(4) Division of language (语言的除法): Divide the price of a dish into several parts to make it seem less expensive. For example: "A shark-fin soup pot is 200 yuan, but the average cost for 10 people is only 20 yuan. You can spend only 20 yuan to taste this delicious dish."

(5) Transition of language (语言转折法): First follow the guest's meaning, then gently turn to another meaning, which maintains the guest's face and promotes the product smoothly. For example: "This dish is really expensive, but its materials are precious, the cooking process is complex, and the taste is unique, so you might try it as well."

(6) By celebrity (借名人之口法): Promote the product through the praise of famous people to make the guests feel it is worth buying. For example: "This is the blunt-snout bream praised by Mao Zedong, you must like it as well," or "Years ago, Emperor Qianlong came to Jiangnan and tasted this dish called 'Beggar's Chicken'. It received high praise for its taste. Would you like to try it?"

(7) Praise method (赞扬法): Use compliments when introducing dishes. For example: "This 'pleurotus eryngii with three-color prawns' is one of our specialty dishes. Would you like to try it?"

(8) Be close to the guest (亲近法): This method is mostly used for promoting to familiar customers. For example: "Today I would like to recommend a good dish, a new specialty of our restaurant. Would you like to try it?"

一、点菜前的准备工作

  1. 了解菜单上菜肴的制作方法、烹调时间、口味特点和装盘要求。
  2. 熟悉菜单上的计量单位，如菜肴的数量。盘的大小、重量或份数通常用作计量单位。
  3. 掌握客源国和地区的饮食习惯和菜肴知识，了解客人口味及饮食需求，以便于给出更好的建议。
  4. 掌握不同人数的客人所需菜品的组成和分量。
  5. 掌握点菜的顺序、时间和调味。

二、中餐点菜的基本服务流程

从形式上看，中餐点菜的基本服务流程比较简单，包括递送菜单、问候客人、点菜、介绍推荐、记录菜名和复述确认。

中餐点菜服务流程与标准
序号服务环节服务标准与要求
1递送菜单一般由引位员（或服务员）将菜单递送给客人。若餐厅通过手机或iPad点菜，服务员应在需要时指导客人如何点菜。
2问候客人问候客人，然后询问是否可以点菜，如"请问现在可以点菜了吗？"
3询问客人是否可以点菜站在客人左侧，身体稍向前倾，认真倾听；礼貌地说"请慢慢看"，并轻声询问"现在可以点菜了吗？"
4点菜耐心等待客人看菜单（或手机/平板点菜），尽量详细地回答客人的提问，耐心向客人解释菜品的原料、口味、制作工艺等。遵循点菜顺序：冷菜先于热菜；荤菜（肉/鱼）先于素菜；特色/地方菜先于普通菜；菜肴先于汤、甜品和酒水。
5介绍推荐服务员应体谅客人的需要，询问客人的喜好和荤素，合理推荐餐厅特色菜、招牌菜。
6记录菜名清晰、准确地记录不同客人所点的菜肴，避免混淆。
7复述确认复述客人所点的菜肴并请客人确认；询问酒水饮料并记录；收回菜单并告知客人大约的等待时间。
8迅速下单迅速下单。填写内容要齐全，菜品与酒水分开，冷菜与热菜分开。部分餐厅使用的电子点菜系统会自动将点菜单联网传至厨房和备菜部，更加方便快捷。

注意事项：若客人点的菜肴已售罄，应致歉并说"抱歉，我们没有……了"，然后推荐其他："您要不要试试……？很美味的。"若某道菜烹制时间较长，应提前向客人说明："恐怕这道菜需要一些时间准备，您介意吗？"注意身体姿态，勿将点菜单放在桌上。

三、点菜服务的方法

在接受客人点菜时，服务员要掌握方法并具备灵活处理问题的能力。点菜服务的方法如下：

（一）常规点菜法：指按冷菜、热菜、酒水、主食的程序进行。

（二）推荐点菜法：指向客人推荐店内的招牌菜、特色菜、时令菜、创新菜等。

（三）推销点菜法：按客人的消费动机来推销：

  • 便餐：来餐厅吃便餐的客人有各种情况——外地出差、旅游、学习的客人就近解决吃饭问题；附近居民因某种情况而来用餐等。他们要求菜点经济实惠、品种不要太多、上菜速度快，所以应主动介绍价廉物美、制作时间短的菜肴。
  • 慕名而来：客人被餐厅的名菜吸引而来。应注意多介绍一些有特色的菜肴，数量上少而精。
  • 宴请：如商务、结婚、祝寿等各类宴请。这类宾客有些讲究排场，需注意菜肴精美。
  • 朋友/同事聚会：客人通常要求气氛热烈、边吃边谈，对菜肴要求不高。一般偏好品种多但不太多、精细而不贵的菜肴。

（四）心理点菜法：按客人的特性来进行推销：

  • 炫耀型：这类客人情绪化，重交情、爱面子，喜欢炫耀财富，要求质量高但不要求上菜快。
  • 茫然型：这类客人很少外出就餐，不知道哪家餐厅好、想吃什么，缺乏相关知识和经验。
  • 习惯型：这类客人偏好某家餐厅的风味、某位厨师的声誉或某一两道菜。服务时应注意打招呼，并询问："××先生，是和上次一样，还是另外点菜？我们饭店推出了您以前没尝过的新菜，是否品鉴一下？"

四、点菜推销的语言技巧

（一）使用选择式问句：用选择性的推销语言询问客人，更容易使客人做出决定。例如："请问您是喝红茶还是绿茶？"而不说"请问您喝茶吗？"

（二）语言的加法：罗列菜品的优点向客人介绍，让客人对菜品感兴趣。例如："这道菜不仅味道好，而且原料十分新鲜，含有多种维生素，还对虚火等症状有辅助疗效。"

（三）语言的减法：向客人说明餐厅特色菜的特点，告诉客人若不品尝将很遗憾。例如："这种银鱼只有太湖一带的水域中才有，您如果现在不品尝，回家后将很难有机会品尝到了。"

（四）语言的除法：将一份菜的价格分为若干份，使其看来不贵。例如："一品鱼翅锅虽然要200元一份，但10个人平均下来不过20元，您只花20元就能品尝到这道美味菜肴了。"

（五）语言转折法：首先顺着客人的意思，然后委婉地转折，既维护客人的面子，又能顺利推销产品。例如："这道菜的价格确实有些贵，但其原料比较名贵，成菜工艺较为复杂，口味别具特色，您不妨一试。"

（六）借名人之口法：借名人的赞美推销自己的产品，让客人感到值得购买。例如："毛泽东曾赞赏过湖北的武昌鱼，您如果品尝一下一定会有同感。"或者"当年乾隆皇帝下江南吃过'叫花鸡'这道菜，对此赞许有加，您是否品尝一下？"

（七）赞扬法：在介绍菜肴时使用赞语。例如："这道'杏鲍菇三色明虾球'是我们这里的招牌菜之一，您不妨尝尝。"

（八）亲近法：多用于对熟悉客户的推销。例如："今天我介绍一道好菜，是我们餐厅最新推出的特色菜肴，您尝尝如何？"

任务实施Task Implementation

Implementation steps

Step 1: The teacher introduces the scenario of this section: During dinner time, a foreign guest Mr. Smith and his friends came to ×× Chinese restaurant for a meal. Mr. Smith is visiting China for the first time and wants the waiter to recommend some special dishes for them.

Step 2: The teacher explains the methods and precautions of ordering food, and lists some common Chinese and English ordering expressions for students to practice and refer to.

Step 3: The teacher gives example cases and demonstrates them.

Step 4: Work in groups (5 persons per group), in which one student acts as the waiter and the other four act as Mr. Smith and his friends. The waiter uses different selling methods of dish-ordering in Chinese and English. (Each group takes turns to carry out scenario simulation, and the selling method cannot be repeated.)
