任务1 结账服务

Task 1: Handling the Bill
案例导入Case Introduction
A guest found that the amount on the bill did not match what he had ordered, so he asked the waiter what was going on. After verification, it was learned that the waiter had taken the wrong bill. Although the matter was finally handled properly, the guest never came back to the restaurant again.
客人发现账单上的金额和自己所点的对不上，于是就问服务员是怎么回事，经过核实后，得知是服务员拿错了账单。虽然事情最后得到了妥善处理，但是客人再也没有来光顾这家餐厅。
思考讨论Discussion
What were the inappropriate aspects of this waiter's performance?
思考讨论：该服务员的做法有哪些不妥之处？
知识准备Knowledge Preparation

Procedures and Standards of Handling Payment

Usually, the bill has to be prepared for the guests when all the dishes are served. The procedures of handling payment consist of three parts: preparing the bill, delivering the bill and handling payment.

Figure 8.1 The Procedures of Check-out Service

(1) Preparing the bill
  • After serving all the dishes, the waiter should check the bill at the cashier's desk. When the guest asks to check out, ask the guest to wait a moment and go to the cashier immediately to fetch the bill.
  • Put the bill into the check book (bill folder) with a pen, and make sure the front of the bill faces the guest when it is opened.
Delivering the bill
  • Walk to the right side of the guest, open the bill folder, hold the bill with the right hand, hold the bottom of the bill with the left hand, and deliver it to the guest saying: "Excuse me, sir/madam. This is your bill, please." Pay attention not to show the bill to other guests.
Handling payment
  • Receive cash or credit card from the guest and send it to the cashier. After giving change or returning the card, thank the guest politely.

Practical Conversation — Go Dutch

(W: Waiter, G: Guest)

W: Excuse me, we are finished here. Could you bring us the bill, please?
W: Please wait a moment. (After a while) Here you are.
G: How much is it in all?
W: 280 yuan in all. It includes a 10% service charge.
G: Fine. But we would like to pay separately. Would you take care of that?
W: No problem. I'll bring separate bills in a minute.
G: Ok. By the way, should we just leave the tips on the table?
W: Thank you for your kindness, but we don't receive tips.

一、结账工作流程与服务标准

通常而言，上菜完毕后即可做结账准备。结账分为三个步骤，分别为结账准备、递送账单和处理付款。

图8.1 结账工作流程 The Procedures of Check-out Service

（一）结账准备 Preparing the bill
  • 给客人上完菜后，餐厅服务员要到收银台核对账单。当客人要求结账时，请客人稍后，立即去收银台取账单。
  • 将账单放入账单夹内，并确保账单夹打开时，账单正面朝向客人，准备好结账用笔。
递送账单 Delivering the bill
  • 走到客人右侧，打开账单夹，右手拿住账单，左手托住账单底部，递送给客人并说："先生/女士，这是您的账单，请您过目。"注意不要让其他客人看到。
处理付款 Handling payment
  • 从客人处接过现金或信用卡送至收银台，找零或还卡后礼貌致谢。

场景对话——分开结账 Go Dutch

（W：服务人员，G：客人）

W：打扰一下，我们吃好了。请帮我们拿一下账单好吗？
W：请稍等，我现在去拿。（过了一会儿）好的，这是您的账单。
G：一共多少钱？
W：一共是280元，其中包含10%的服务费。
G：好的。但是我们想要分开付账。你可以帮我们弄吗？
W：没问题。我待会儿会把你们每个人的账单送过来。
G：好的。顺便说一下，我们把小费留在桌上就可以了吗？
W：感谢您的关照，不过我们不收小费。

任务实施Task Implementation

Step 1: The teacher sets up the scenario. Students work in groups to simulate the practice.

Step 2: The teacher summarizes the key points of the service process.

Step 3: Peer evaluation.

第一步：教师设置场景，学生以小组为单位进行模拟练习。

第二步：教师总结服务过程中的要点。

第三步：进行同伴评价。

任务2 付款方式

Task 2: Payment Methods
案例导入Case Introduction
A small problem occurred during the service: the guests were from the United States and were used to paying by card. When the guest took out an American Express card, the restaurant did not accept it and the waiter had never seen it before, so Xiao Li bluntly told the guest that the hotel did not accept this card and asked the guest to use another card. The guest agreed and gave him a Visa card. But when Xiao Li swiped the card, the POS machine malfunctioned and could not process it. Ten minutes passed and the guest, still waiting for Xiao Li to return, became impatient and went to the front desk to ask. He learned that the restaurant's POS machine was out of order and Xiao Li had to go to another restaurant in the hotel to swipe the card, which caused such a long delay. When Xiao Li hurried back and handed the swiped card and receipt to the guest, explaining why he had been delayed, the guest still left the restaurant in a displeased mood. Xiao Li also wore a very helpless expression.
服务中出现了一点小问题：客人来自美国，他们习惯用刷卡的方式结账，但当客人拿出美国运通卡时，由于这个酒店不接纳此卡，且小李也未见过此卡，因此小李很粗暴地告知客人酒店不接受此卡，并请客人换其他的卡付款。客人同意，并给了他一张维萨卡。但小李在刷卡的时候，POS机又出现了故障，导致一直刷不出来。10分钟过去了，客人还是未等到小李回来，便有些不耐烦了，走到前台去询问，得知本餐厅的POS机故障，小李只能去酒店其他餐厅刷卡，所以耽误了这么长时间。当小李着急地跑回来，将刷好的卡和单据交到客人手上，并解释自己为什么耽误这么长的时间时，客人却依然带着不悦的心情离开了餐厅。小李这时也露出很无奈的表情。
思考讨论Discussion
When problems occur during payment, how should the server communicate and explain to the guest?
思考讨论：当结账出现问题时，如何与客人沟通解释？
知识准备Knowledge Preparation

Common Methods of Payment

Currently, cash, credit card, charge to room, mobile payment apps and check are popular payment methods among customers.

(1) By cash
  • When the guest pays in cash, the waiter should check the cash in front of the guest before sending it to the cashier. Ask the guest to wait a moment, and double check whether the amount of change and the bill are correct.
  • The waiter should put the top copy of the bill, the change and the invoice into the bill folder, return to the right side of the guest, open the folder and hand it to the guest.
  • If the guest insists on checking out at the front desk in person, lead the guest to the cashier politely.
(2) By credit card
  • First of all, confirm whether the restaurant accepts the category of the credit card. If yes, send it with the bill to the cashier and ask the guest to wait a moment politely.
  • Double check the amount and make sure it is correct on the receipt and bill. Put them into the bill folder with the credit card and present them to the guest, asking the guest to check and sign the bill and credit card receipt. Check if the signature is consistent with the credit card.
  • Return the first page of the bill, the guest stub of the credit card receipt and the credit card. Send the remaining bills and stubs back to the cashier.
(3) Charged to the room account

This method is appropriate for guests who live in the hotel, units that have a contract with the hotel, senior managers of the hotel and VIP guests of the restaurant.

  • For hotel guests, ask them to show their room cards politely.
  • Deliver the pen, and ask the guests to write down the room number.
  • Check with the front desk to make sure the account balance is enough to cover the bill. If yes, present the bill to the guests and ask them to sign. Otherwise, tactfully state the fact and ask them to change the method of payment.
(4) By mobile app

Guide the guests to use Alipay or WeChat to scan the QR code of the restaurant to process payment.

(5) By check

This method is appropriate for big companies, grand banquets or tourist groups.

  • If guests would like to pay the bill by check, ask them to show their ID card or employee's card with the check politely, and send them together with the bill to the cashier.
  • The cashier goes through the procedures including filling the check, recording the certificate number (ID or employee's card) and their telephone number.
  • Double check the items and the amount, send the first page of the bill, the check stub, the certificate and the invoice back to the guest, and sincerely thank him/her for his/her presence.

二、常见的付款方式

目前，常见的付款方式主要有现金结账、信用卡结账、签单结账、手机App结账和支票结账等。

（一）现金结账 By cash
  • 客人付现金时，服务员要礼貌地在餐桌旁当面点清钱款，请客人稍候；将账单及现金送给收银员后，核对找回的零钱及账单上联是否正确。
  • 服务员将账单上联和所找零钱、发票夹在账单夹内，返回客人右侧，打开账单夹，递送给客人。
  • 如果客人坚持要到前台亲自结账，礼貌地引领客人到收银台。
（二）信用卡结账 By credit card
  • 首先确认是否是本餐厅接纳的信用卡，然后请客人稍候，并将信用卡和账单送至收银处。
  • 服务员检查无误后将收据、账单及信用卡夹在账单夹内，交还给客人，请客人在账单和信用卡收据上签字，并检查签名与信用卡上一致。
  • 将账单第一联、信用卡收据客人存根及信用卡交还给客人；将剩余账单及存根送回收银台。
（三）签单结账 Charged to the room account

这一方式适用于：住店客人、与酒店签订合同的单位、酒店高层管理人员及酒店的VIP客人等。

  • 如果是住店客人，礼貌地要求客人出示房卡。
  • 递上笔，示意客人写清房号。
  • 与总台核对，确认账户余额足够支付。若足够，将账单呈给客人并请其签字；否则委婉说明情况并请客人更改付款方式。
（四）手机App结账 By mobile app

指导客人用手机打开支付宝或微信等App，扫描本店的二维码进行付款。

（五）支票结账 By check

这一方式适用于：大企业、大公司的订餐或大型宴会、旅游团队用餐。

  • 如客人用支票结账，请其出示身份证或工作证，连同支票、账单一并交给收银员。
  • 收银员办理结账手续，填写支票、记录证件号及联系电话。
  • 服务员核对后，将账单第一联、支票存根、证件、发票一齐送还给客人，并真诚感谢客人。
实用表达Useful Expressions
(1) Enquiry of payment
  • Can I have the bill, please? / Let me have the bill, please. / Bring me the bill, please. / May I have the bill, please? / The bill please. / Bring me the check, please.
  • How would you like to pay the meal? / How would you like to make the payment? / How would you like to settle the bill?
  • Pay in cash / By credit card / By traveler's checks / Charge it to my room.
  • Would you like to pay together or separately? / Separate bills or one for all? / A check for each or a check for all?
  • A Dutch treat / Go Dutch.
  • Would you like a breakdown of the bill?
  • You may either pay in cash, with credit card, check or charge it to your room.
  • I'd like to pay by credit card instead of in cash.
  • Do you accept foreign currency?
(2) The change
  • Here is your change, sir. Thank you for coming.
  • Here is your receipt and change.
  • Keep the change, please.
  • We are happy to serve you, but we do not accept tips.
（一）询问结账方式
  • 请把账单给我。/请结账。
  • 请问您打算用什么方式结账？
  • 用现金、信用卡、旅行支票、计入房账。
  • 请问是分开结账还是一起结账？
  • AA制。
  • 您的账目要细分吗？
  • 您可以付现金，或者用信用卡、支票支付，或者计入房账。
  • 我想用信用卡代替现金付款。
  • 您接受外币吗？
（二）找零
  • 这是找回的零钱，先生。感谢您的光临。
  • 这是您的收据和找回的零钱。
  • 不用找零了。
  • 我们很乐意为您服务，但我们不能接受小费。
任务实施Task Implementation

Step 1: The teacher introduces the settings of this section: Mr Wang finished his meal with his friends. In handling the payment, the waiter found the credit card balance was not enough to cover the bill. What would you do if you were the waiter? Think of more than one solution and use proper language to perform the service.

Step 2: The teacher lists related vocabularies, phrases or sentence patterns in English for students' reference.

Step 3: The teacher gives cases and demonstrates.

(W: Waiter, G: Guest)

G: Excuse me, please!
W: Yes, sir. Do you want something else?
G: No. Bring me the check, please.
W: Ok, please wait a moment. Here is your bill, sir. How would you like to make the payment?
G: By credit card. (Take out the card)
W: (Accept the card) Yes, sir. Just a minute. (Leave to the cashier)
W: (Return the credit card and go back to the guest, speak gently) Excuse me sir. The balance is not enough. Would you like to change another card or pay by other methods?
G: (Take out another card) Try this one, please.
W: Ok, thank you. (Walk back to the seat) It's all done, sir. Here is your receipt, please keep it well.

Step 4: Three students form a group, in which one student plays the waiter, the other two act as guests. Simulate the situation with proper English.

第一步：教师导入本节情景设定：王先生和朋友在酒店用完餐，用信用卡结账，刷卡时服务员发现信用卡额度不足，此时他该怎么处理？请考虑多种结果，并使用英文为王先生提供结账服务。

第二步：教师列出结账用到的相关英文词汇、短语或句型，以供学生参考。

第三步：教师给出示范案例，并予以示范。

（W：服务人员，G：客人）

G：服务员！
W：是的，先生。您还有别的需要吗？
G：没有了，请帮我结账。
W：好的，请稍等。先生，这是您的账单，请问您打算如何付款？
G：刷卡。（拿出信用卡）
W：（接过信用卡）好的，先生，请稍等。（离开到收银台）
W：（送回卡片，走到客人身边轻声说）不好意思先生，卡里余额不足，您要换一张卡片还是改成其他方式结账？
G：（拿出另一张卡）试试这张吧。
W：好的，谢谢。（折回座位）先生，办好了。这是您的收据，请收好。

第四步：以小组（三人一组）为单位，其中一位学生扮演服务员，另外两位学生扮演结账客人，模拟结账环节的情景。

任务3 突发事件处理

Task 3: Dealing with Accidents
案例导入Case Introduction
Xiao Wang accompanied a manager to entertain a client for a meal. The boss was a gourmet and a regular customer of that restaurant, so the waiter did not need to introduce much; the restaurant arranged everything quickly. Xiao Wang quickly checked the bill, verified it was correct, and paid by card according to the amount on the bill, and reminded the manager to issue an invoice. At this moment, the client suddenly asked Xiao Wang: "How much did this meal cost?" Xiao Wang suddenly did not know how to answer, and looked at the boss. The boss's face darkened and the atmosphere became heavy. The manager immediately steered the topic away: "Everyone, I am the manager of the restaurant, this is my business card, please give me your advice, and welcome to come again." As the manager spoke, he handed his card to the guests. The atmosphere gradually improved, and the manager left politely. Afterwards, Xiao Wang visited the restaurant manager to express his gratitude, and decided to hold all future business banquets at this hotel.
小王陪同一位经理宴请客户就餐。老板是个美食家，也是那家餐厅的常客，因此无须服务员多作介绍，餐厅便很快安排妥当。小王快速地查看了账单，核对无误后按照账单上的金额刷卡结账，并提醒经理开具发票。这时，客户突然问小王："这顿饭花了多少钱？"小王一下子不知道该怎么回答才好，便看向老板。此时老板的脸色也沉了下来，气氛一下子凝重起来。经理一见马上将话题引开："各位，我是餐厅的经理，这是我的名片，请各位多指教，欢迎以后多来。"经理一边说着一边将名片递给各位客人。气氛渐渐好转，经理才礼貌地出去。事后，小王拜访餐厅经理表示感谢，并将以后的商务宴请都定在了这家酒店。
思考讨论Discussion
Facing various unexpected situations, how should the server respond?
思考讨论：面对各种突发事件，服务人员该如何应对？
知识准备Knowledge Preparation

Dealing with Accidents

The problems that hotel staff may encounter will vary from guest to guest. Whatever the situation is, quality service should be the priority. As accidents may occur without a ready-made or clear solution, a well-trained staff will try to solve the problem with wisdom, professional tactics and creativity. If you are not sure about the situation, do not hesitate to ask your colleague for help.

(1) Enquiries on the bill

Misunderstanding of the bill may occur because the guests misread the price when making the order. The waiter should bring the menu and ask the guest to check it, and patiently explain the items to the guests. In some cases, the waiter is responsible for the mistake. Apologize to the guests and offer a discount or compensation, such as coupons, to gain the guests' understanding before settling the bill.

(2) Watch out for the guests leaving without paying

Occasionally, guests may leave without paying the bill. Watch out for the following situations to prevent it from happening:

  • A single diner who comes to the restaurant for the first time may leave with an excuse of going to the toilet or answering the phone.
  • Pay special attention to the table with expensive orderings. Normally, as there is a budget for corporate entertaining, the host may order expensive dishes to balance the expense.
  • Watch out for the table where the guests leave successively with different reasons and never come back.

二、突发事件处理

酒店员工可能遇到的问题会因客人而异。不管是什么样的情况，服务是首要任务。许多情况没有现成或明确的解决方法，此时酒店员工便要利用判断、专业和创造力。如果不确定如何处理，应该果断寻求同事的协助。

（一）客人对账单持有疑义

客人在点单时，有时会看错价格，在结账时对金额有疑义。此时，服务员应拿来菜单请客人核对，并认真、耐心地向客人解释账单。在有些情况下，是服务员的责任造成错误，应道歉并给客人优惠或补偿，获得客人谅解后再结账。

（二）防止客人逃账

偶尔会有客人未结账就离开。应注意以下情况预防其发生：

  • 首次来餐厅的单身客人可能以去洗手间或接电话为由离开。
  • 特别注意点了昂贵菜品的餐桌。通常因公宴请有预算，会点高价菜来平衡开支。
  • 一桌客人席间分别以各种理由离席且未再返回。
常见中英文表达Common Expressions
(1) Check and explain the bill
  • Thank you for waiting, Mr Brown. Here is your bill. Would you like to check it?
  • That's much more than I expected.
  • Shall I explain some items for you?
  • How much is the total?
  • Can you give me a discount?
  • How about the service charge?
  • May I have an invoice?
  • Certainly, sir. May I know the name of your company?
  • Does it include all the expenses?
  • Your bill includes a 10% tax and a 10% service charge.
  • I'm sorry, discount cards and coupons cannot be used at the same time.
  • Here are some coupons for you. You can pay with them next time if you have dinner in our restaurant. Please pay attention to the instructions and expiry date.
(2) Others
  • 100 yuan for four drinks which includes 8% service charge.
  • There is no cover charge for seats in the bar.
  • That's our minimum charge.
  • May I make a copy of your card?
  • I'm sorry but we don't accept personal checks according to the hotel policy.
  • We accept US dollars, but we only have RMB for change.
  • I regret to tell you that this bill is a forged one.
  • There is a discount for children under 5 years old.
  • I'm awfully sorry. The cashier miscalculated the bill by adding the bill of another table to yours.
  • Please pay at the cashier on your way out.
  • One of the beers should have been deducted from your bill.
（一）核实及解释账单
  • 让您久等了，布朗先生。这是您的账单。您需要核对一下吗？
  • 那比我预料中的要超出许多呢。
  • 需要我解释一些项目吗？
  • 总共多少钱？
  • 你能给我一个折扣吗？
  • 服务费呢？
  • 我可以要发票吗？
  • 当然可以，先生。请问贵公司名称是什么？
  • 请问所有的费用都包含在里面了吗？
  • 您的账单包含10%的税和10%的服务费。
  • 抱歉，折扣卡和赠券不能同时使用。
  • 我们有一些赠券送给您，下次您在我们餐厅用餐时可以使用。请留意使用说明和有效期。
（二）其他
  • 100元4份饮料，包括8%的服务费。
  • 酒吧里不收座位费。
  • 这是我们的最低消费。
  • 我能复印一下您的卡吗？
  • 对不起，根据酒店政策我们不接受个人支票。
  • 我们接受美元，但只能用人民币找零。
  • 我很遗憾地告诉您这张钱是假币。
  • 5岁以下的儿童有折扣。

  • 请您在前台付款。
  • 其中一份啤酒应该从您的账单中扣除。

任务4 送客服务

Task 4: Seeing off Guests
案例导入Case Introduction
It is crucial to show our appreciation when the guests leave the restaurant, which is usually ignored, but will leave a full stop for the journey and leave a lasting impression. An experienced waiter will observe guests' needs to offer service at a proper time, which will surprise their guests and provide service beyond expectations.
在客人离开餐厅时表达我们的谢意至关重要，这一点常被忽视，却会给客人的旅程画上一个圆满的句号，给人留下持久的印象。有经验的服务员会观察客人的需求，在恰当的时机提供服务，给客人带来惊喜，提供超预期的服务。
思考讨论Discussion
Which service details moved the guest?
思考讨论：是哪些服务细节感动了客人？
知识准备Knowledge Preparation

Procedures and Standards of Seeing off Guests

(1) See off guests
  • When the guests finish their meal and are ready to leave, move the chairs for them under the principle of "guests first" and "ladies first".
  • Remind the guests to take their belongings. Look around to make sure no private stuff is left.
  • Bid farewell to guests with courtesy and see them off to the doorstep of the restaurant.
  • Call the lift for the guests if the restaurant is on the upper floor. See them off until the lift door is closed.
Clean the table
  • After the guests have gone, check if they left their belongings behind.
  • Change used tableware and replace them with the new ones.
  • Change the tablecloth, set the table, and adjust the setting of chairs.

一、送客人离开餐厅服务流程及规范

（一）送别客人
  • 客人起身时，服务员须主动为客人搬开座椅，并按先宾后主、女士优先的原则。
  • 提醒客人带好随身物品，环顾四周再次确认。
  • 礼貌地向客人道别，并送客人至餐厅门口。
  • 如客人乘坐电梯，服务员应为客人叫梯，送客人进入电梯后，目送客人离开。
整理台面
  • 客人离开餐厅后，服务员须检查台面上下有无客人遗忘的物品。
  • 更换用过的餐具，换上新的。
  • 更换台布，重新摆台，并调整座椅。
实用表达Useful Expressions
  • Please don't leave anything behind.
  • Please mind / watch your step.
  • Please be careful.
  • Thank you for coming, goodbye.
  • Goodbye, hope to see you again.
  • Have a nice trip!
  • Wish you a pleasant journey!
  • 请带好您的随身物品。
  • 请注意脚下。
  • 请当心。
  • 再见，谢谢您的光临。
  • 再见，期待再次见到您。
  • 一路平安！
  • 旅途愉快！

拓展阅读Extended Reading

Notes on Handling Payment

  1. Bills that are altered or soiled must not be presented to guests.
  2. After the bill is presented, if it is not yet paid, pay close attention to prevent the guest from leaving without paying.
  3. Count the change clearly in front of the guest; for foreign guests, it may help to add up the amounts to show how the change is calculated.
  4. Banknotes carry bacteria; after handling them, do not touch your eyes, mouth or food with your fingers.
  5. Payment methods include cash, charge to room, check or credit card; because their procedures differ, their associated risks should be noted.
  6. When paying in foreign currency, state the exchange rate and amount clearly, fill in an exchange memo, then ask the guest to sign and provide the passport number.
  7. For a resident hotel guest's charge, ask to see the room card, register the room number and verify the guest list, then present the bill for signature and transfer it to the hotel front desk.
  8. A service charge and a tip are different in nature: the service charge is calculated at a fixed percentage and usually added into the bill, while a tip is a discretionary reward given by the guest for the service provided.
1. Why must altered or soiled bills never be presented to guests?
Altered or soiled bills are unprofessional and may cause disputes; only clean, correct bills should be given to guests.
2. What should a server do to prevent a guest from leaving without paying after the bill is presented?
Pay close attention to the guest and the table to prevent "dine and dash"; watch for excuses to leave and verify payment before they go.
3. How should a server handle the change when a foreign guest pays?
Count the change clearly in front of the guest, and for foreign guests use addition to show how the change is calculated.
4. What is the difference between a service charge and a tip?
A service charge is a fixed-percentage amount added to the bill; a tip is a discretionary gratuity the guest gives voluntarily for the service.
5. What steps are required when a resident hotel guest wishes to charge the bill to the room?
Ask for the room card, register the room number, verify the guest list, present the bill for signature, then transfer it to the front desk.
